### [1] [What Makes Creation Human? Authorship, Reasons, and Meaningful Human Control in Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10738)
*Yuxi Cao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文区分"作品层面的创造性"与"创作者层面的创作主体性"，指出人类作者身份不能仅凭人工干预程度、自动化程度、初始想法来源或最终选择权来判定，而取决于人的判断与理由是否真正塑造了作品的发展。为此，作者将"有意义的人类控制"（MHC）引入生成式创作，指出其经典"追踪条件"的局限，并提出"动态反思性追踪"（DRT）：创作者不断演化的理由需经历反思性接纳、对创作轨迹产生真实影响，并能拒绝和重定向系统的默认方向。DRT包含四个条件——历时性理由形成、反思性接纳、轨迹效力、可争辩性与可重定向——外加一项最低限度的可追溯要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI大幅扩展了创作者的生产能力，但生产能力的提升并不必然带来创作主体性或作者身份的增加。现有关于人类作者身份的判定标准（如人工干预、自动化程度、初始想法来源、最终选择权）存在不足，需要一套更恰当的标准来解释：在生成式AI参与创作时，人类作者的资格究竟取决于什么。同时，经典MHC的追踪条件预设理由在交互前已完全确定，无法适用于创作理由在人机交互中生成、变化或放弃的情形。

Method: 概念分析与理论建构。首先区分作品层面的创造性（creativity of the work）与创作者层面的创作主体性（creative agency of the creator）；其次论证人工干预、自动化程度、初始想法来源与最终选择权均不能单独决定人类作者身份；随后引入"有意义的人类控制"（MHC）框架，指出其经典追踪条件在生成式创作中的局限；最后提出"动态反思性追踪"（DRT）作为替代方案，并将其细化为四项条件：历时性理由形成、反思性接纳、轨迹效力、可争辩性与可重定向，附加一项最低限度的可追溯要求。

Result: 论证得出：人类作者身份取决于人的判断与理由是否真正塑造作品的发展，而非人亲自执行的步骤数量；作者身份的判断标准应从"过程性参与量"转向"理由是否持续、反思性且有效地塑造作品的最终形态"。DRT提供了一套可操作的判据集合，用以评估生成式AI创作情境下人类作者身份的成立与否。

Conclusion: 在生成式AI条件下，人类作者身份不取决于个人亲自完成了多少步骤，而取决于其理由是否持续地、反思性地、有效地塑造了作品所成为的样子。DRT及其四项条件与最低可追溯要求，为判定生成式创作中的人类作者身份提供了理论标准，也为AI创作工具的伦理与规范设计提供了依据。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) significantly expands creators' productive capacity, but this does not necessarily entail a corresponding increase in creative agency or authorship. This paper distinguishes creativity at the level of the work from creative agency at the level of the creator, and argues that human authorship cannot be determined solely by manual intervention, degree of automation, the origin of an initial idea, or final selection authority. Rather, authorship depends on whether human judgment and reasons genuinely shape the development of the work.
  To articulate this requirement, the paper introduces Meaningful Human Control (MHC) into generative creation and identifies a limitation of its classical tracking condition. Creative reasons are not always fully specified prior to interaction with AI; they may emerge, change, or be abandoned as the creative process unfolds. The paper therefore proposes dynamic-reflexive tracking (DRT), which requires that a creator's evolving reasons undergo reflective uptake, exert genuine influence on the subsequent trajectory of creation, and remain capable of rejecting and redirecting the system's default direction.
  DRT consists of four conditions: diachronic reason formation, reflective uptake, trajectory efficacy, and contestability and redirection, together with a minimal tracing requirement. The paper argues that human authorship under generative AI depends not on how many steps a person personally performs, but on whether that person's reasons continuously, reflectively, and effectively shape what the work becomes.

</details>


### [2] ["Coder first, advocate second, college student third": The Liminality of Going to College as a Blind Computing Student](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10942)
*Isabela Figueira,Josahandi M. Cisneros,Stacy M. Branham*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过访谈十名盲或低视力（BLV）大学生，探讨其进入大学学习计算机专业过程中的转型经历，揭示数字无障碍与大学适应障碍如何迫使他们承担"第二职业"——盲人倡导者，或退出计算机专业，从而解释了该领域"漏水管线"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲或低视力学生大学毕业率偏低，在计算机领域尤为明显。已有研究记录了高中和大学阶段的无障碍挑战，但对导致"漏水管线"的过渡过程缺乏理解，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 访谈十名准备进入大学学习计算机的BLV大学生，并以"交叉阈限性"（Intersecting Liminality）这一人生过渡视角对数据进行分析。

Result: 部分BLV学生面临极其严重的数字无障碍和大学适应障碍，以至于作为程序员继续前行的唯一方式是额外承担一份"第二职业"——充当盲人倡导者，否则只能退出计算机专业。

Conclusion: 大学过渡期是分析与技术干预的关键节点；交叉阈限性为HCI学者提供了一个有用视角，用以剖析阻碍BLV学生完成计算机学位的叠加性挑战。

Abstract: Blind or low vision (BLV) students are less likely to graduate from college, particularly in computing. Prior work documents accessibility challenges in high school and college, but we lack understanding of the transition process that produces this "leaky pipeline." To address this, we interviewed ten BLV college students about going to college to study computing. We analyzed our data through the lens of life transition, specifically Intersecting Liminality. Our findings reveal that some BLV students face such immense digital accessibility and college acclimation barriers that the only way forward as coders is to take on a "second job" as a blind advocate or drop out of the computing major. We argue that the college transition is a critical point for analysis and technological intervention, and further, that Intersecting Liminality provides a useful lens for HCI scholars to unpack the compounding challenges that prevent some BLV students from completing computing degrees.

</details>


### [3] [ShellVis: Sandboxed Live Programming for Shell Scripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11000)
*Joshua Horowitz,Jeffrey Heer*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出通过沙箱技术在有副作用的环境中实现实时编程，并以ShellVis（面向shell脚本的实时编程工具）作为案例验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时编程可让程序员在编辑程序时即时运行和追踪，从而获得可见性。但对于具有潜在有害副作用的程序，实时性会把错误变成灾难，因此需要一种机制在保留实时编程优势的同时避免真实副作用。

Method: 用沙箱实现有副作用环境中的实时编程：将副作用限制在真实环境的模拟中。具体应用于shell脚本场景，开发ShellVis工具，对脚本运行时行为提供逐行反馈，并通过文件系统的安全覆盖层（overlay）对文件操作进行沙箱化。采用定性用户评估。

Result: 用户评估表明ShellVis对参与者有帮助，替代了繁琐的既有做法并增强了信心；参与者反馈还揭示了未来研究方向，尤其是需要在缩小评估鸿沟（gulf of evaluation）的同时缩小执行鸿沟（gulf of execution）。

Conclusion: ShellVis展示了沙箱如何将实时编程技术引入到众多副作用至关重要的真实编程场景中，是一个可推广的案例研究。

Abstract: Live programming provides visibility to programmers by running and tracing programs as they are edited. However, for programs with potentially harmful side effects, liveness can turn mistakes into disasters. We propose enabling live programming in environments with side effects via sandboxing: confining effects to a simulation of the true environment. We apply sandboxed live programming in the challenging context of shell scripting: a ubiquitous and powerful---yet notoriously opaque and error-prone---tool. ShellVis provides line-by-line feedback on a shell script's run-time behavior, with file operations sandboxed via a safe overlay of the file system. A qualitative user evaluation finds ShellVis to be helpful to participants, replacing tedious existing practices and instilling confidence. Participant responses also reveal areas for future research, particularly bridging the gulf of execution alongside the gulf of evaluation. ShellVis serves as a case study of how sandboxing can bring live-programming techniques into the many real-world programming contexts where side effects are important.

</details>


### [4] [X-Hinges: 3D Printing Self-Sensing Compliant Mechanisms for Continuous and Multi-DOF Motion Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11077)
*Xiang Chang,Haiyang Yan,Stefanie Mueller,Jiaji Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: X-Hinges：基于多材料FDM 3D打印的自感知柔性机构设计与制造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在柔性机构中嵌入传感能力通常需要后期装配，难以实现单次打印成型的多自由度连续运动感知。

Method: 共打印两种不同电导率的导电丝材于柔性体内，直接嵌入电阻式传感元件；设计支持三自由度、每自由度配专用传感元件；开发精密数据采集系统与数据驱动回归模型，并提供交互式设计工具。

Result: 实现无需后装配的连续实时多自由度运动感知；设计工具可定制几何、力学性能、自由度与传感配置，并能增强现有3D模型；展示个性化交互界面应用案例。

Conclusion: X-Hinges为一体化自感知柔性机构及个性化交互界面制造提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present X-Hinges, a design and fabrication method for self-sensing compliant mechanisms based on multi-material FDM 3D printing. By co-printing two conductive filaments of different conductivities within a compliant body, we embed resistive sensing elements directly during fabrication without post-assembly, enabling continuous motion sensing across multiple degrees of freedom in a single print. The structure supports three degrees of freedom, each equipped with a dedicated sensing element configuration for multi-DOF motion estimation. We develop a precision data acquisition system and data-driven regression models that enable continuous, real-time motion sensing. We also introduce an interactive design tool for customizing the geometry, mechanical properties, degrees of freedom, and sensing configurations of X-Hinges. The tool also supports augmenting existing 3D models with self-sensing structures, endowing ordinary objects with continuous multi-DOF sensing capabilities. Finally, we present a set of application examples demonstrating the capability of X-Hinges for fabricating personalized interactive interfaces.

</details>


### [5] [Visual-Motion-Induced Modulation of Pedestrian Trajectories Using Spatially Distributed Multi-Display Signage in Public Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11088)
*Yuri Mikawa,Taiki Fukiage,Yuki Kubota,Takumi Yokosaka,Maki Ogawa,Kazushi Maruya*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于知觉的多屏标牌（MDS）行人轨迹侧向调控方法，利用视动错觉（vection）产生的横向移动单色条纹，诱导行人姿态反应以偏转行走方向，并通过实验室实验与博物馆实地部署验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多屏标牌已普遍存在于城市环境中，具备影响公共空间人类行为与体验的潜力，但其独特能力（呈现空间分布式的动态视觉刺激）目前主要用于广告。作者希望探索其作为非语言手段引导行人在公共空间中行走的可能性，即通过视觉刺激侧向调制行人轨迹。

Method: 研究以视动错觉（vection，自我运动的错觉）为动机，采用视动研究中的标准刺激——横向移动的单色条纹，通过空间分布的多块显示屏呈现，以诱发可能偏转行人轨迹的姿态反应。研究采用两种方法：1）受控实验室实验，检验MDS装置是否在既有研究预测的方向上引起轨迹偏移；2）真实世界实地部署，在国家博物馆的实际行人流中考察无约束运动条件下是否出现类似的聚合行为效应。

Result: 实验室中，全屏运动显著使行走轨迹向视觉运动方向偏转，而部分条纹运动未产生显著方向效应。实地部署中，方向相反的全屏运动条件在聚合行人位置上产生了方向一致的差异。尽管真实行人流变异性较大，实地结果仍扩展了受控实验室发现，为基于MDS的行人调控提供了具生态效度的证据。结果还提示，足够的视觉运动覆盖范围可能是重要因素。

Conclusion: 基于视动错觉的多屏标牌可用于非语言地侧向引导行人轨迹，实验室与实地证据一致支持该方法的可行性；视觉运动覆盖范围（全屏vs部分）对效果至关重要，这为公共空间中实用的MDS行人调控提供了依据。

Abstract: Multi-display signage (MDS), now ubiquitous in urban environments, has the potential to influence human behavior and experience in public spaces. However, despite its unique capability to present spatially distributed dynamic visual stimuli, its current use is mainly limited to advertising. In this study, we propose a perception-based approach for laterally modulating pedestrian trajectories as a nonverbal means of guiding pedestrians in public spaces. The approach is motivated by vection, the illusion of self-motion, and uses laterally moving monochrome stripes, a standard stimulus in vection research, presented across spatially distributed displays to elicit postural responses that may bias pedestrian trajectories. We evaluated the approach through a controlled laboratory experiment and a real-world field deployment involving actual pedestrian flows in a national museum. The laboratory experiment examined whether the MDS setup induced trajectory shifts in the direction predicted by prior research on the behavioral effects of vection. The field deployment investigated whether comparable effects would emerge in aggregate pedestrian behavior during unconstrained movement under conditions closer to those of urban public spaces. In the laboratory, full-screen motion significantly biased walking trajectories in the direction of visual motion, whereas partial-stripe motion produced no significant directional effect. In the field deployment, opposing full-screen motion conditions produced direction-consistent differences in aggregate pedestrian positions. The field results, observed despite the substantial variability in real-world pedestrian flows, extend the controlled laboratory findings and provide ecologically valid evidence supporting practical MDS-based pedestrian modulation in public settings. The results further suggest that sufficient visual-motion coverage may be important.

</details>


### [6] [How AI Coders Discuss, Disagree, and Reach Consensus: Challenges and Opportunities for LLM-Based Qualitative Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11109)
*Jeongyeon Kim,John Mitchell*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文量化了多智能体大语言模型在定性编码中的有效性，揭示影响编码结果的关键情境与结构因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在多编码者定性编码中的效用虽被广泛讨论，但缺乏实证证据界定其可靠应用的场景。

Method: 构建基于文献的基线流程，使AI智能体独立编码、辩论并协调分歧，并在多样化定性数据集上评估多智能体LLM编码效果。

Result: 编码准确性取决于编码本长度、定性数据相似性及智能体分歧程度；智能体间激烈且未解决的辩论反而带来更高准确性；LLM模仿了多种人类讨论行为，但缺乏对情境的自适应响应。

Conclusion: 提出自动化编码系统的设计建议，并公开AI讨论数据集与方法框架，为AI驱动的自动化主题分析设计奠定基础。

Abstract: The utility of AI in multi-coder qualitative coding has been widely discussed, yet little empirical evidence exists to delineate the contexts in which it performs reliably. We address this gap by quantifying the effectiveness of multi-agent LLM coding across varied qualitative datasets, revealing key contextual and structural factors that mediate coding outcomes. We developed a literature-informed baseline pipeline that enables AI agents to independently code, debate, and reconcile disagreements. Results revealed that coding accuracy depends on factors such as codebook length, qualitative data similarity, and agent disagreement. Notably, intense and unresolved debates between agents led to higher accuracy. Our analysis showed that while LLMs emulate many human discussion behaviors, they lack adaptive responsiveness to context. From these findings, we offer design recommendations for building automated coding systems. Our open-source AI discussion dataset and methodological framework lay the groundwork for advancing the design of AI-mediated automated thematic analysis.

</details>


### [7] [Conceptualising an Initial Design Space for Guidance in Digital Physical Activity Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11193)
*Faith Young,Markus Tatzgern,Alexander Meschtscherjakov,Jan Smeddinck*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出数字身体活动支持中"引导（guidance）"的初始设计空间，将其定义为情境化、面向行动的支持，并沿多个维度刻画其特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引导常被视为数字健康干预的关键能力，但缺乏统一定义，且与个性化、反馈、说服等概念混淆，限制了理论清晰度与设计进展。

Method: 综合行为改变、人机交互、具身认知与数字健康等文献，概念化并提出引导的设计空间，从范围、目的、时机、情境、模态、具身性、适应性、自主性、情感质量等多维度刻画。

Result: 提出引导的明确定义及多维设计空间框架，提供结构化的概念词汇与理论基础。

Conclusion: 该工作旨在支持更一致的研究、比较及负责任的数字健康干预设计，为身体活动支持中的引导提供概念基础。

Abstract: Providing guidance is frequently referenced as a key capability of digital health interventions targeting physical activity, yet the term remains poorly defined and inconsistently applied. Existing work often conflates guidance with related constructs such as personalisation, feedback, or persuasion, limiting both theoretical clarity and design progress. This paper conceptualises an initial design space of guidance in the context of digital physical activity support. We define guidance for physical activity as situated, action-oriented support that scaffolds users' embodied engagement in physical activity. Drawing on literature from behaviour change, human-computer interaction, embodied cognition, and digital health, we outline a design space that characterises guidance along multiple dimensions: scope, purpose, timing, context, modality, embodiment, adaptivity, autonomy, and affective quality. By offering a structured vocabulary and conceptual foundation, this work aims to support more coherent research, comparisons, and responsible design of digital health interventions featuring guidance for physical activity support.

</details>


### [8] [AI Soccer Analyst: Stage-Aware and Verifiable Human-AI Collaboration for Soccer Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11224)
*Calvin Yeung,Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出 AI Soccer Analyst——一个具有可修订阶段（数据理解、问题定义、结构化规划、执行、基于证据的报告、交互与精炼）的混合主动人机协作系统，用于体育数据分析，实现可检查、可修订、可验证的分析流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体育数据分析师需将领域问题转化为洞察，需结合计算与体育领域专业知识。大语言模型虽简化编程，但"提示到报告"的流程可能掩盖决策依据与证据，缺乏可验证性与人类控制。

Method: 首先通过 5 名分析师的形成性研究确定设计目标（自动化、可验证性、人类控制、可访问性）；随后进行基于任务的评估，16 名参与者，结合系统日志、留存产物、评分与开放式反馈。

Result: 48 项任务中 33 项达到操作完成标准。经 Holm 校正后，探索性检验支持参与者对完成任务的输出质量、任务达成、可靠性与可验证性的积极评价。交互记录显示领域知识通过澄清、规划与精炼环节涌现。

Conclusion: 研究结果说明，阶段感知的人机协作是产出可检查、可修订、可验证分析的实用方法，同时能在关键决策中保留领域专家的参与。

Abstract: Sports data analysts translate domain questions into insights by combining computation with sport-specific domain expertise. Large language models ease programming, but prompt-to-report workflows may obscure decisions and evidence. We present AI Soccer Analyst, a mixed-initiative system with revisable stages: Data Understanding, Problem Definition, Structured Planning, Execution, Evidence-Grounded Reporting, and Interaction and Refinement. A formative study with five analysts first informed design goals for automation, verifiability, human control, and accessibility. Subsequently, a task-based evaluation with 16 participants combined system logs, retained artifacts, ratings, and open responses; 33 of 48 tasks met the operational completion criteria. Exploratory tests supported favorable participant perceptions of completed-task output quality, task achievement, reliability, and verifiability after Holm correction. Interaction records showed domain knowledge emerging through clarification, planning, and refinement. These findings position stage-aware human-AI collaboration as a practical approach for producing inspectable, revisable, and verifiable analyses while retaining domain-expert involvement in consequential decisions.

</details>


### [9] [Are Caption Metrics Broken? Latency, Deaf and Hard of Hearing User Ratings, and Bias across Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11408)
*Bernard Thompson,James Waller,Luz Fanny Calderon Torres,Lu Ming,Mariana Arroyo Chavez,Dante Conway,Raja Kushalnagar,Christian Vogler*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 一项包含216名有效参与者的美国大规模在线调查发现，电视与ASR字幕在观众评分上相近，但WER、ACE2、NER三项指标仅对电视字幕与观感有中高相关，对ASR字幕相关性低，且字幕延迟（尤其是7-12秒）显著影响观看体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电视直播字幕常存在错误和时序问题，影响听障（DHH）观众的观看体验，因此需要评估现有的字幕质量指标是否能真实反映DHH观众的实际观看感受。

Method: 开展美国大规模在线调查，216名有效参与者共提供302份回应、累计4832个数据点；参与者观看来自70个直播电视片段的视频，在四种条件下（原始电视字幕，延迟达7-12秒；与音频同步的电视字幕；与音频同步的ASR字幕；平均延迟两秒的ASR字幕）对字幕质量和内容主观理解进行评分，并将所有字幕用WER、ACE2和NER三项指标进行评估。

Result: 电视字幕与ASR字幕获得的观众评分相似；对电视字幕，三项指标与观众评分呈中到高相关，但对ASR字幕相关性明显更低，说明这些指标并非技术中立；此外字幕延迟显著影响观看体验，尤其是典型的7-12秒电视延迟。

Conclusion: 现有字幕质量指标不能同等适用于不同来源的字幕技术，且在评估中需考虑延迟因素；论文讨论了这些发现对字幕质量指标采用与推广的启示。

Abstract: Live captions on TV often contain errors and timing issues, making it hard for deaf and hard-of-hearing (DHH) viewers to follow dialog. It is essential that caption quality metrics reflect the lived DHH TV viewing experience. To this end, we describe a U.S.-based large-scale online survey with 216 validated participants, who provided 302 responses containing a cumulative 4,832 data points. Participants viewed videos drawn from a pool of 70 clips recorded from live TV, and were asked to rate the caption quality and subjective understanding of the content across four conditions: TV captions as originally recorded with up to 7-12 seconds delay, TV captions synchronized with audio, Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR)-generated captions synchronized with audio, and ASR captions with an average two-second delay. All captions were evaluated against the Word Error Rate (WER), Automated Caption Evaluation (ACE2) and Number, Edition and Recognition (NER) metrics. Results show that TV and ASR captions were rated similarly. For TV captions, all three metrics were moderately-to-highly correlated with viewer ratings, but far less so for ASR captions, making them far from technology-neutral. Additionally, caption latencies significantly impact the viewer experience, especially typical 7-12-second TV delays. We discuss the implications for the adoption of caption quality metrics.

</details>


### [10] [Design Reflections on Transition to LLM-Aided Novel Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11503)
*Richard Brath*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究基于12.5年、55个可视化的单一被试设计记录，回顾近3.75年大语言模型对数据可视化设计演化的影响，发现LLM降低了编码成本、拓展了设计空间，并改变了设计流程与设计者体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大语言模型进入设计实践后，数据可视化设计在长期演化中发生了何种变化，尤其是LLM如何影响设计空间探索与设计流程。

Method: 采用纵向个案（single-subject）设计，收集长达12.5年、共55个可视化的语料，对其中近3.75年LLM介入阶段的演化进行回顾性分析。

Result: LLM有助于设计空间探索：减少编码投入、带来新的设计机会，并引发冲击、兴奋、成就感以及设计流程的转变。

Conclusion: 大语言模型不仅是效率工具，更重塑了可视化设计的机会空间与设计过程，同时带来显著的情感与工作方式变化。

Abstract: This study examines data visualization design evolution over 12.5 years, reflecting on the impact of Large Language Models over the last 3.75 years. Using a longitudinal corpus of 55 visualizations from a single-subject design record, the study identifies how LLMs have aided design-space exploration: reducing coding effort, enabling new design opportunities, shock, excitement, accomplishments, and shifts to the design process.

</details>


### [11] [Ethics Training Agents: Facilitating Group-Based Ethics Education with Role-Playing and Discussion for Ethical Reflection and Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11529)
*Youngseok Seo,Sueun Jang,Hyesoo Park,Renz Samuel Gutierrez,Joseph Seering,Uichin Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Ethics Training Agents 系统，利用多个具有不同伦理取向的 LLM 智能体及主持人智能体，支持人机群体伦理讨论，并通过 45 名 STEM 本科生的用户研究验证其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向 STEM 学生的群体伦理培训是一项复杂挑战，需要大量资源与专业知识。现有基于活动的教学（如角色扮演、讨论）虽能模拟真实情境，但多依赖人工操作，且缺乏与有效在线平台结合以支持群体伦理讨论。

Method: 作者设计了 Ethics Training Agents——一个群体讨论系统，引入多个代表不同伦理取向的 LLM 参与者以及一个主持人智能体，从而开展结构化的人机群体伦理讨论，促进协作式反思。随后开展了一项包含 45 名 STEM 本科生的用户研究，评估学习效果与用户体验。

Result: 结果表明，该系统能够支持群体讨论中的参与度、协调性与观点采择，并对伦理敏感性产生积极影响。

Conclusion: 论文还讨论了将多个 LLM 智能体整合进多人群体场景以促进 STEM 学生伦理培训的实践设计策略。

Abstract: Group-based ethics training for Science, Technology, Engineering and Mathematics (STEM) students is a complex challenge, requiring substantial resources and expertise. While activity-based teaching methods, such as role-playing and discussions, are commonly employed to simulate real-world scenarios, current practices are often manual and lack integration with effective online platforms for supporting group-based ethical discussions. In this work, we propose Ethics Training Agents, a group discussion system that leverages multiple LLM participants embodying distinct ethical orientations, along with a moderator agent, to enable structured human-AI group ethical discussions for collaborative reflection. We conduct a user study with 45 undergraduate STEM students to evaluate the learning outcomes and user experience. The results show that our system supports engagement, coordination, and perspective-taking in group discussions and has a positive influence on ethical sensitivity. We also discuss practical design strategies for integrating multiple LLM agents into multi-human group settings to facilitate ethics training for STEM students.

</details>


### [12] [Bigger than the EAR BOX: A Theory-Grounded Review of XR Accessibility Research for Deaf and Hard of Hearing Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11706)
*Shuxu Huffman,Michaela Okosi,Jose Merino,Tifanie Bouchara,Abraham Glasser,Christian Vogler,Raja Kushalnagar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文对2015—2025年ACM发表的53项涉及听障（DHH）用户的XR研究进行理论驱动的综述，基于残障研究、聋人研究与DeafSpace构建四维分析框架，揭示该领域多将XR无障碍定位于"听觉默认环境中的沟通支持"，忽视聋人群体的多样性，并提出重新分配无障碍劳动与重构空间的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR技术在听障群体无障碍研究中日益受到关注，但支撑这类工作的潜在假设（如无障碍的取向、责任归属、对聋人群体的理解及空间与感知预设）较少被审视。作者希望以理论视角揭示并反思这些隐含假设。

Method: 理论驱动的文献综述。整合Disability Studies、Deaf Studies与DeafSpace，提出包含四个分析维度的理论框架：无障碍取向、责任分配、DHH社区的概念化、空间与感知假设。据此对通过检索与筛选获得的2015—2025年ACM出版物中53项涉及DHH用户的XR研究进行分析。

Result: 分析显示，XR无障碍常被框定为在"听觉默认"环境中支持沟通，而忽视了DHH社区的多样性；同时识别出重新分配无障碍劳动与重构空间的相关研究方向。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了批判性的理论框架，可作为未来涉及DHH社区的XR研究的分析工具，推动从"补偿听觉环境"转向更尊重聋人多样性与空间需求的研究取向。

Abstract: Extended Reality (XR) technologies have received growing attention in accessibility research involving Deaf and Hard of Hearing (DHH) communities. Yet less attention has been given to the assumptions shaping this work. We present a theory-grounded review of XR research involving DHH users. Drawing on Disability Studies, Deaf Studies, and DeafSpace, we develop a theoretical framework with four analytical dimensions: orientation toward access, distribution of responsibility, conceptualization of DHH communities, and spatial and perceptual assumptions. We apply this framework to 53 XR studies involving DHH users, identified through a search and screening of ACM publications from 2015 to 2025. Our analysis shows that XR accessibility is frequently framed as supporting communication within hearing-default environments, while overlooking the diversity of DHH communities. We identify directions for redistributing accessibility labor and reconfiguring space, and offer our framework as a tool for future XR research involving DHH communities.

</details>


### [13] [Understanding Operator Attitudes Toward AI-Supported Decision Making in Maritime Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11805)
*Doreen Jirak,Armeen Saroukanoff,Dirk van Rooy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过问卷调查与开放回答分析，研究海事利益相关者对AI辅助避碰系统的态度，发现态度总体积极，信任稳定，解释评价更敏感，并强调应以“校准依赖”为核心设计人机协作系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水面船舶（MASS）与AI决策辅助系统有望变革海事作业，但其安全落地取决于海事专业人员如何感知与信任这些系统，因此需要实证了解其态度与关键影响因素。

Method: 采用问卷调查：让参与者针对避碰场景中的AI辅助助手进行评价，测量技术焦虑、自动化信任与解释质量；并结合对开放式回答的情绪分析与主题分析。

Result: 参与者对海事技术总体持积极态度；开放性与年龄无明确差异；跨场景信任较稳定；解释质量评分呈多维度且对场景更敏感。开放回答显示，参与者重视决策支持、态势感知与信心提升，同时担忧AI可靠性、过度依赖与专业技能丧失。

Conclusion: 海事AI系统不应只追求提高自动化水平或增强信任，而应通过透明、可靠、具有操作意义的设计，并在领域中引入专家参与，支持“校准依赖”，从而实现安全整合。

Abstract: Maritime Autonomous Surface Ships (MASS) and AI- supported decision assistants are expected to transform maritime operations, but their safe integration depends on how maritime professionals perceive and trust such systems. This paper presents a survey study on maritime stakeholders' attitudes toward an AI-supported assistant in collision-avoidance scenarios. Participants evaluated technology anxiety, trust in automation, and explanation quality using established and adapted questionnaires, complemented by sentiment and thematic analysis of open-ended responses Results indicate a generally positive disposition toward maritime technology, no clear age-related differences in openness, stable trust across scenarios, and more scenario-sensitive, multidimensional explanation ratings. Open responses showed that participants valued support for decision-making, situation awareness, and confidence-building, while raising concerns about AI reliability, over- reliance and loss of expertise. The findings suggest that maritime AI systems should not focus solely on increasing automation or trust, but on supporting calibrated reliance through transparent, reliable, and operationally meaningful design with domain experts in the loop.

</details>


### [14] [MotionQ: Operator-Conditioned Motion Quotients for Cross-Observation WiFi Gesture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11818)
*Xiang Zhang,Huan Yan,Geying Yang,Jianchun Liu,Tao Liu,Zhi Liu,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文研究WiFi手势识别在用户朝向、链路或收发器布局变化时的性能退化问题，指出这些变化会改变无线观测算子，并据此提出MotionQ方法，通过算子条件化的双支撑运动度量和单链路保留干预提升跨观测算子泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WiFi手势识别在固定部署下较准确，但当用户朝向、可用链路或收发器位置变化时性能下降。现有方法多追求域不变特征，忽视布局和观测配置变化，且这些变化实际上改变了无线观测算子，使得同一动作产生不同测量并改变可物理观测的任务相关运动线索。

Method: 在WiFi前向过程的局部线性化下，推导出一个共同任务可观测条件，说明何时可从每个由几何引起的算子中恢复严格的共同线性表示并保持手势任务。当条件不满足时，强行在更多异构源算子上对齐可能丢失仍可观测的任务线索。为此提出MotionQ：为每个候选几何生成算子条件化的双支撑运动度量；用运动商消除其无标签支撑的任意排序，并以置换不变的中心矩表示；不跨算子匹配商，而采用单链路保留干预，促使每个视图保留足够手势识别信息。

Result: 大量评估表明，MotionQ对外推性观测算子具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 改变观测算子不仅改变固定线索的外观，还改变可物理观测的任务相关运动线索；因此不应一味追求跨算子不变表示。MotionQ通过算子条件化表示与单链路保留干预，在观测算子变化和外推场景下实现更稳健的WiFi手势识别。

Abstract: WiFi gesture recognition is accurate in fixed deployments but often degrades when user orientation, available links, or transceiver placement changes. Unlike ordinary domain shifts, these changes alter the wireless observation operator, so the same motion is expected to produce different measurements. Existing methods nevertheless pursue domain-invariant features and largely overlook changing layouts and observation configurations. Yet changing the observation operator also changes which task-relevant motion cues are physically observable, rather than merely altering the appearance of a fixed set of cues. Under a local linearization of the WiFi forward process, we derive a common task-observability condition under which a strict common linear representation is recoverable from every geometry-induced operator while preserving the gesture task. When the condition fails, enforcing stronger alignment across additional heterogeneous source operators may discard task-relevant cues still observable under individual operators. We therefore present MotionQ, which generates an operator-conditioned two-support motion measure for each candidate geometry. A motion quotient removes only the arbitrary ordering of its unlabeled supports and is represented by permutation-invariant central moments. Rather than matching quotients across operators, single-link-retention interventions encourage each view to retain information sufficient for gesture recognition. Extensive evaluations show that MotionQ is robust to extrapolative observation operators.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [15] [Probabilistic Focal Search: Accelerating Bounded-Suboptimal Search via Lower-Bound Advancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10584)
*Minh Vu Duc,Trung Le Huu,Hà Minh Hoàng,Trung Thanh Nguyen,Phuong Khanh Nguyen,Huynh Thi Thanh Binh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出概率焦点搜索(PFS)，以概率p进行启发式引导、以1-p扩展最小f节点，加速有界次优搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 焦点搜索(FS)虽有启发式引导，但确定性策略可能长期不改变f_min，延缓FOCAL接纳新节点，限制求解进度。

Method: PFS以概率p沿用FS引导选择，以概率1-p扩展OPEN中最小f节点以推进下界；并将该调度机制迁移到动态势搜索得到PDPS，以及任何时候扩展APFS。

Result: 在N-Puzzle、煎饼排序、TSP及GCTSP上测试。当f_min长期停滞时收益最大，节点扩展可减少约90%以上；APFS在GCTSP上优于所有对比算法，煎饼排序等确定性搜索高效场景收益较小。

Conclusion: 概率因子在FOCAL接纳成为瓶颈时最有效，且该机制可迁移至势引导，但效果依赖领域与界值。

Abstract: Bounded-suboptimal search seeks a solution within a factor $w$ of optimal while reducing search effort. Focal Search (FS) uses heuristic guidance within FOCAL, the frontier nodes eligible under the threshold $w f_{\min}$, but its deterministic policy may leave $f_{\min}$ unchanged for many expansions. We introduce Probabilistic Focal Search (PFS), which follows the FS guided choice with probability $p$ and expands a minimum-$f$ OPEN node with probability $1-p$. The latter branch encourages the lower bound to advance, enlarging FOCAL and admitting nodes that may lead to feasible solutions. By balancing guidance and lower-bound advancement, this mechanism can reduce time to a bounded solution when progress is limited by delayed FOCAL admission. As a secondary transfer experiment, we apply the same scheduler to Dynamic Potential Search, yielding Probabilistic Dynamic Potential Search (PDPS). We benchmark PFS against FS on N-Puzzle, Pancake Sorting, and the Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP), and evaluate its anytime extension on the Generalized Covering TSP (GCTSP), using multiple $w$ and $p$ values. Across these benchmarks, the largest gains occur when long $f_{\min}$ plateaus delay useful FOCAL admissions; in such settings, the probabilistic factor may reduce node expansions by about 90\% or more (e.g., on N-Puzzle and TSP). For the anytime algorithm family, Anytime Probabilistic Focal Search (APFS) outperforms all tested algorithms in evaluating anytime methods on GCTSP. We also observe that the benefit is smaller when the deterministic search already advances efficiently (e.g., Pancake Sorting), indicating that the probabilistic factor is most useful when FOCAL admission is a search bottleneck. The PDPS transfer shows that the mechanism also transfers to potential guidance, although its common-success effects remain domain- and bound-dependent.

</details>


### [16] [Automating Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) Formulation Generation from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10629)
*Niloy Kumar Mondal,Md Rizwan Parvez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出端到端多智能体框架，自动将自然语言问题描述转化为QUBO形式，并发布QUBOBench基准，准确率达68%，较单次调用基线提升22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QUBO是组合优化的核心形式，与量子及量子启发求解器兼容。但将自然语言问题描述转化为正确的QUBO形式十分困难，需要识别二进制变量、约束、目标函数、惩罚项及合适的惩罚权重，过程耗时且依赖大量领域专业知识。

Method: 提出端到端多智能体框架，从自然语言问题描述自动生成QUBO形式，支持结构化与非结构化测试用例；同时构建QUBOBench基准，涵盖12个应用领域的100个组合优化问题，问题来自同行评审文献、竞赛及经典NP难问题。

Result: 框架在QUBOBench上达到68%的准确率，比直接单次调用基线高出22%。进一步分析表明，迭代自我修复是提升性能的最重要组件。

Conclusion: 多智能体框架能有效自动化QUBO形式生成，迭代自我修复是关键，数据与代码已开源。

Abstract: Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) is a central formulation for combinatorial optimization and has gained increasing attention due to its compatibility with quantum, hybrid quantum-classical, and quantum-inspired solvers. However, translating natural-language problem descriptions into correct QUBO formulations remains difficult, requiring the identification of binary variables, constraints, objective functions, penalty terms, and suitable penalty weights. This process is time-consuming and often demands substantial domain expertise. To address this challenge, we propose an end-to-end multi-agent framework that automatically generates QUBO formulations from natural-language problem descriptions, supported by structured or unstructured test cases. To evaluate its performance, We also introduce QUBOBench, a benchmark containing 100 combinatorial optimization problems across 12 application domains, curated from peer-reviewed literature, competitions, and canonical NP-hard problems. Experimental results show that our framework achieves 68% accuracy on QUBOBench, outperforming a direct single-call baseline by 22%. Further analysis identifies iterative self-repair as the most important component contributing to improved performance. The data and code are open-sourced at https://quitttcat.github.io/QuantumQUBOAgent.

</details>


### [17] [A Multi-Stage Rule-Chaining Framework for Compositional and Interpretable Cognitive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10654)
*Deblina Kar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向ARC基准的多阶段规则链式框架，通过符号、结构与概念三个层次的组合推理，在ARC-AGI-2任务上取得超过95%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARC基准用于评估认知泛化能力，即从有限示例中推断并应用抽象规则的能力。现有方法在可解释性与跨层次抽象推理方面仍有不足，作者希望构建一个无需任务特定调优、透明且可泛化的推理架构。

Method: 框架整合三个互补求解器并顺序运行于渐进式回退层级中：（1）确定性规则发现模块，通过几何、颜色和对象分析归纳原子变换；（2）模式组合引擎，通过块合并、重复和空间启发式重建输出；（3）结构抽象层，推断网格间的层次与嵌套关系。每一阶段复用先前的推理轨迹以增强可解释性与泛化能力。

Result: 在1000个任务中995个通过训练，在120个任务中进一步评估105个，并在240个ARC-AGI-2任务中解决了230个测试任务。系统在确定性、组合性和抽象类别上均实现较强覆盖，整体准确率超过95%。

Conclusion: 该架构连接了符号推理与模式合成，为认知泛化提供可解释的洞见。结果表明规则链式与层次化组合可推动机器推理朝着透明、人类对齐的抽象方向发展，且不依赖任务特定调优。

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC) benchmarks cognitive generalization, the ability to infer and apply abstract rules from limited examples. This paper presents a multi-stage rule-chaining framework that performs compositional reasoning across symbolic, structural, and conceptual levels. The framework integrates three complementary solvers:
  (1) a deterministic rule discovery module that induces atomic transformations through geometric, color, and object-based analysis;
  (2) a pattern-composition engine that reconstructs outputs via block merging, repetition, and spatial heuristics; and
  (3) a structural abstraction layer that infers hierarchical and nested relationships across grids.
  These solvers operate sequentially within a progressive fallback hierarchy, where each stage reuses prior reasoning traces to enhance interpretability and generalization. Training passed for 995 tasks out of 1000, further evaluated on 105 tasks out of 120 and solved 230 test tasks out of 240 ARC-AGI-2 tasks. The system achieved strong coverage across deterministic, compositional, and abstract categories, demonstrating an overall accuracy exceeding 95 percent. The proposed architecture bridges symbolic reasoning and pattern synthesis, providing interpretable insight into cognitive generalization. The results suggest that rule chaining and hierarchical composition can advance machine reasoning toward transparent, human-aligned abstraction without relying on task-specific tuning.

</details>


### [18] [Understanding LoRA Rank Trade-offs in Diffusion Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10656)
*Iman Khazrak,Narges Nejad,Mostafa M. Rezaee,Robert C. Green*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验研究扩散模型微调中LoRA秩的选择，发现秩4在DDPM U-Net上取得最佳FID，小到中等秩在固定训练预算下更具效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择LoRA秩需要在生成质量与计算成本之间进行权衡，但缺乏系统的实证指导。本文旨在明确不同秩对扩散模型微调效果的影响，为实践提供可复现的默认设置建议。

Method: 在CIFAR-10上使用DDPM U-Net，秩设为{2,4,8,16,32}，固定优化设置并采用可复现的本地pytorch-fid协议，报告FID、可训练参数、运行时间与GPU内存；并用扩展预算DDPM（20轮，秩4/8/16）和Tiny DiT骨干（10轮，秩4/8/16）验证趋势。

Result: 中等秩效率最高：秩4取得最佳DDPM FID（124.1380），秩8接近（124.2136）；更高秩虽然适配成本更大，但收益有限。

Conclusion: 在固定训练预算下，小到中等秩（如4–8）是扩散模型LoRA微调的实用默认选择。

Abstract: Selecting LoRA rank for diffusion fine-tuning requires balancing quality and compute cost. We present a controlled study on CIFAR-10 using a DDPM U-Net with ranks {2,4,8,16,32}, fixed optimization settings, and a reproducible local-folder pytorch-fid protocol. We report FID, trainable parameters, runtime, and GPU memory, then validate trends with extended-budget DDPM runs (20 epochs; ranks 4/8/16) and a Tiny DiT backbone (10 epochs; ranks 4/8/16). Results show moderate ranks are most efficient: rank 4 achieves the best DDPM FID (124.1380), rank 8 is close (124.2136), and higher ranks provide limited gains despite larger adaptation cost. These findings support small-to-moderate ranks as practical defaults under fixed training budgets.

</details>


### [19] [Quantifying the Memorization-to-Generalization Transition: Scaling Laws and Phase Structure in Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10657)
*Anish Kataria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过384组双层MLP模运算实验，刻画了grokking(从记忆到泛化的延迟转变)在超参数空间中的定量边界，拟合出泛化起始时间的幂律标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: grokking现象的"为何发生"已有理论进展，但其"何时发生"在超参数空间中的定量结构尚未被刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在模运算任务上训练384种配置的双隐层MLP，映射记忆到泛化的边界，对泛化起始时间拟合幂律标度关系并引入交互项，同时分析权重衰减相边界与权重范数轨迹。

Result: 得到标度律T_grok ∝ H^-0.27 D^-2.04 η^-0.50 λ^-0.64 (R²=0.732，含交互项0.821)；指数层级显示数据复杂度(D^-2.04)是主导因素而非模型容量(H^-0.27)，数据翻倍加速泛化约4倍而宽度翻倍仅约1.2倍；权重衰减λ≳1.0处存在清晰相边界，转变期间权重范数单调压缩。

Conclusion: 研究为预测和控制过参数化网络中的机制转变提供了定量基础。

Abstract: Neural networks trained past memorization frequently undergo a delayed transition to generalization, a phenomenon known as grokking. Despite theoretical progress on \emph{why} this transition occurs, the quantitative structure of \emph{when} it occurs in hyperparameter space remains uncharacterized. We map the memorization-to-generalization boundary across 384 configurations of two-hidden-layer MLPs on modular arithmetic, fitting a power-law scaling relation for generalization onset time: $T_{\mathrm{grok}} \propto H^{-0.27}\, D^{-2.04}\, η^{-0.50}\, λ^{-0.64}$ ($R^2 = 0.732$; $0.821$ with interactions). The exponent hierarchy reveals that data complexity ($D^{-2.04}$) is the dominant driver of regime transition, not model capacity ($H^{-0.27}$): doubling data accelerates generalization by ${\sim}4\times$, while doubling width yields only ${\sim}1.2\times$. A sharp phase boundary at weight decay $λ\gtrsim 1.0$ separates grokking from non-grokking configurations, and weight norm trajectories show monotonic compression during the transition, consistent with implicit regularization selecting low-complexity solutions. These results provide a quantitative foundation for predicting and controlling regime transitions in overparameterized networks.

</details>


### [20] [An Open Recipe for IMO Gold: Training Nemotron for Olympiad Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10712)
*Ivan Moshkov,Stephen Ge,George Armstrong,Wei Du,Sadegh Mahdavi,Igor Gitman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探索了后训练与测试时推理设计对自然语言奥数证明生成的影响，基于Nemotron 3 Ultra训练了两个专家模型，并构建了一个纯自然语言的测试时计算流水线，在IMO 2026上取得30/42分，达到金牌门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索模型后训练与测试时推理设计如何影响高难度奥数数学的自然语言证明生成，并推动开源模型在无形式化证明器、无外部工具、无网络条件下解决奥赛级数学问题。

Method: 以Nemotron 3 Ultra为起点，通过监督微调与强化学习训练两个专家检查点；评估检查点选择、验证与修正策略；据此构建一个完全基于自然语言的测试时计算流水线，由通用模型与两个后训练专家模型组成迭代搜索，负责生成、验证、修正候选证明，再由独立的高算力阶段选出最终提交答案。

Result: 系统在IMO 2026上获得42分中的30分，达到金牌阈值；同时发布两个后训练检查点、训练数据、训练与推理代码、提交解答，以及包含200道全新奥赛级题目的Nemotron-IMO-Bench基准。

Conclusion: 结合后训练专家模型与测试时迭代搜索及最终选择，可在纯自然语言条件下实现奥赛金牌水平的形式化证明生成，并通过开源模型、数据、代码与基准推动相关研究。

Abstract: We study how model post-training and test-time inference design affect natural-language proof generation for hard olympiad mathematics. Starting from Nemotron 3 Ultra, we train two specialist checkpoints using supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, and evaluate checkpoint choice, verification, and refinement. Based on these findings, we present an open-model test-time-compute pipeline. The system operates entirely in natural language, with no formal prover, external tools, or internet access. Three Nemotron 3 Ultra checkpoints - the general-availability model and two post-trained specialists - power an iterative search that generates, verifies, and refines candidate proofs; a separate high-compute stage then selects each final submission. The system scored 30 out of 42 points at IMO 2026, reaching the gold-medal threshold. We release the two post-trained checkpoints as well as the training data, the training and inference code, the submitted solutions, and Nemotron-IMO-Bench, a new benchmark of 200 novel olympiad-level problems.

</details>


### [21] [Finishing the Task Is Not Enough: Evaluating Agent Resilience and Considerate Participation under Accumulating Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10724)
*Yuanchen Bai,Zijian Ding,Angelique Taylor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"运营韧性"与"体谅性参与"两个评估维度，通过对120条模拟医疗轨迹的研究，考察生成式AI智能体在挑战累积下的行为变化，并归纳出五个部署困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI智能体的持续部署不仅需要完成孤立任务，还需在重复交互、环境变化和共享工作流的人际依赖中保持有用性。然而在挑战累积条件下，智能体的运营韧性与体谅性参与两方面研究仍不充分。

Method: 研究120条模拟医疗轨迹，涵盖两种生成式AI模型和十二项源自利益相关者的任务，设置轻度、中度、重度三级累积挑战，并对比文本行动计划、提示式内部评估以及量化的结构化工作量与情感报告三种方式。

Result: 在运营韧性方面，智能体从自主恢复转向更依赖人类，结构化报告显示工作量和负面情感上升，但文本回应中很少表达压力；在体谅性参与方面，智能体从任务聚焦型适应扩展为任务重构、关注他人、角色边界调整和更广泛的协调，且在行动与内部评估中呈现不同模式。

Conclusion: 研究归纳出涉及持续性、注意力、角色边界、状态披露和升级机制的五个部署困境，需由利益相关者加以明确，并为学习、情境化评估和具身适应提供技术启示。

Abstract: Sustained deployment of generative AI agents requires more than isolated task success. Agents must remain useful across repeated interactions, changing conditions, and dependencies on people within shared workflows, especially as technical, human, and operational disruptions accumulate over time. We propose operational resilience and considerate participation as two complementary aspects of evaluating such agents: the former captures how agents recover from blocked work while preserving progress and communicating their limits, and the latter captures how their adaptation accounts for affected people, role boundaries, and the surrounding workflow. Yet both remain underexplored under accumulating challenge. We study 120 simulated healthcare trajectories across two generative AI models and twelve stakeholder-derived tasks under light, medium, and heavy challenge. We compare textual action plans, prompted internal assessments, and quantitative structured workload and affect reports to examine how agent behavior and reported state change as challenge accumulates. Regarding operational resilience, agents shift from self-directed recovery toward greater human dependence, while reporting increasing workload and negative affect in structured reports but seldom expressing strain in textual responses. Regarding considerate participation, agents broaden from task-focused adaptation toward task reframing, attention to others, role-boundary adjustment, and wider coordination, with distinct patterns across actions and internal assessments. From these findings, we derive five deployment dilemmas involving persistence, attention, role boundaries, state disclosure, and escalation that require stakeholder specification, further informing technical implications for learning, situated evaluation, and embodied adaptation.

</details>


### [22] [Towards a Deterministic Math Solver for Clinical Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10728)
*Felipe Ocampo Osorio,Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordoñez,Maximin Lange,Rafi Al Attrach,Sahil Kapadia,Zakaria Laouabdia Sellami,Angelo Antonio Talio,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Program-Solve接口：让大模型不直接算数，而是编写针对具体病例的Python代码，由受限本地执行器作为确定性求解器执行；在MedCalc-Bench Verified上对比直接算术与手写库，发现该方案对32B模型有显著增益，对7B模型不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在算术上不可靠，而临床计算器中单个数值错误就会改变建议。传统做法是逐个将计算器硬编码为已验证函数，成本高、难扩展。作者希望测试一种替代方案：模型不负责计算，只负责决定如何调用确定性求解。

Method: 提出Program-Solve接口：模型生成病例专属的Python代码，由受限的本地执行器运行作为确定性求解器，模型任务退化为决定如何使用它。在MedCalc-Bench Verified（1,100例、55个计算器）上评估，对比直接模型算术和手写22个计算器库；使用Qwen2.5-7B与Qwen2.5-32B-AWQ；并依据现行临床指南审计基准公式，标记55个中16个存在版本、用途或系数问题。实验在提供公式与金标准变量、两条路径均读取完整病历的条件下进行。

Result: 在7B模型上，交给求解器并非可靠优势（75.31% vs 72.02%，配对+3.29分，95%计算器聚类区间[-3.49, 10.38]）；在32B模型上则构成优势（90.53% vs 83.47%，+7.05 [0.47, 14.60]，区间明确不含零）。手写库在其支持的440例上完全准确，但在其他病例上弃答，整体仅40.0%。

Conclusion: 加入执行器对部分开源权重模型的帮助大于其他模型，即便在公式、变量与病历访问均匹配的条件下也是如此；但它既不能替代经过验证的公式，也不能替代可靠的变量抽取。此外，基准中16/55的计算器公式存在临床规范性问题，提示结果解读需谨慎。

Abstract: Large language models are unreliable at arithmetic, which is a problem for clinical calculators where a single numerical error changes the recommendation. The standard response is to hardcode each calculator as a validated function, one at a time. We test an alternative: the model does not calculate. Instead, it writes case-specific Python that a restricted local executor runs as a deterministic solver, and the model's task reduces to deciding how to use it. We evaluate this Program-Solve interface on MedCalc-Bench Verified (1,100 cases, 55 calculators) against direct model arithmetic and a hand-written 22-calculator library, using Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen2.5-32B-AWQ, after auditing the benchmark's formulas against current clinical guidelines and flagging 16 of 55 with version, use or coefficient concerns. With formulas and gold variables supplied and both routes reading the whole note, handing off to the solver is not a reliable advantage at 7B (75.31% against 72.02%, a paired +3.29 points with a 95% calculator-cluster interval of [-3.49, 10.38]) but is one at 32B (90.53% against 83.47%, +7.05 [0.47, 14.60], clear of zero). The hand-written library is exact on its 440 supported cases but abstains elsewhere (40.0% overall). Adding an executor thus helps some open-weight models more than others even under matched formula, variable and note access, and is not a substitute for verified formulas or reliable variable extraction either way.

</details>


### [23] [Studying Without a Syllabus: Task-Agnostic Environment Preprocessing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10824)
*Vinay Samuel,Varun Ursekar,Vijay S. Kalmath,Apaar Shanker,Veronica Chatrath,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究"任务无关的环境预处理"：智能体在测试前、不知下游任务分布的情况下，以有限预算探索陌生环境并产出可复用产物（索引、脚本、流程指引），供冻结的求解器使用。在六个异构基准上，元智能体变体在五个基准取得最高 Avg@3，固定语料处理在最大语料基准最优；更大预算未必提升下游回报，但产物可减少测试时采样次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化适配方法依赖任务示例、轨迹或评估反馈来决定构建什么，而任务无关方法虽免监督却需预先锁定针对某类环境的准备策略。作者希望研究更开放的设定：智能体能否在没有"教学大纲"、不清楚下游任务分布的情况下，自行研究并决定如何准备陌生环境。

Method: 论文形式化了"任务无关环境预处理"问题：一个研究系统在预算约束下探索环境，为冻结的求解器生成产物。实验中对比了无辅助与带档案的元智能体，以及固定的合成练习法和语料处理法，覆盖六个异构基准。

Result: 元智能体变体在五个基准上取得最高 Avg@3 奖励；固定语料处理在最大语料基准上仍最优。更大的研究预算并未可靠地提升下游奖励；但研究产出的产物能减少达到既定分数所需的测试时采样次数。

Conclusion: 可复用的准备能将计算从反复的测试时尝试转移到任务前的研究阶段，说明智能体可在无任务先验下自主准备环境，且其价值主要体现在提升测试时效率而非单纯提高得分。

Abstract: Before an LLM agent tackles tasks in a new environment, it can inspect available corpora and tools and construct reusable resources such as indices, scripts, or procedural guidance. Most automated adaptation methods, however, rely on task examples, trajectories, or evaluation feedback to decide what to build. Existing task-agnostic approaches avoid this supervision but commit in advance to a preparation strategy for a particular type of environment. We study a more open-ended setting: can an agent study an unfamiliar environment without a syllabus, i.e. before test time and without knowledge of the downstream task distribution, and choose how to prepare it? We formalize task-agnostic environment preprocessing, in which a studying system explores an environment under a budget and produces artifacts for a frozen solver. We compare unaided and archive-equipped meta-agents with fixed synthetic-practice and corpus-processing methods across six heterogeneous benchmarks. A meta-agent variant achieves the highest Avg@3 reward on five benchmarks, while fixed corpus processing remains best on the largest corpus benchmark. Larger study budgets do not reliably improve downstream reward. Nevertheless, studied artifacts reduce the test-time sampling needed to reach a given score, demonstrating how reusable preparation can shift computation from repeated test-time attempts to a pre-task study phase.

</details>


### [24] [When Validation Stops Learning: Auditing Update Admission for Continual Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10873)
*Qinzhen Ma,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出更新准入应同时通过误差控制与保留学习机会双指标审计，识别出区间型置信门在给定交互预算下无法认证旧任务行为不变，并采用配对二项式构造降低该负担，辅以认证历史参考提升与轮级错失机会指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立评估虽能拒绝有害策略更新，但也会阻碍有用的持续学习；因此需要在中立交互预算下，同时衡量误差控制与保留的学习机会，以审计更新准入机制。

Method: 识别区间型置信门的失败模式；用标准配对二项式构造在结果分歧稀少时降低认证负担；设计认证历史参考提升机制与轮级错失机会度量；在32个种子的一步推挤诊断中对比新鲜配对检查与区间门；并开展独立的学习动力学压力测试以区分模型偏差与反馈选择误差。

Result: 在每阶段2000回合预算下，新鲜配对检查准入常见更新流的31.6%，区间门为零；无条件下回放在闭环运行中学习效果更好；学习动力学压力测试能区分模型偏差与反馈选择误差。

Conclusion: 贡献是一套准入审计协议及分析与合成证据；物理机器人与VLA验证仍待开展。

Abstract: Independent evaluation can reject harmful policy updates yet also prevent useful continual learning. We argue that update admission must be assessed through both error control and retained learning opportunities at a stated interaction budget. We identify a concrete failure: a range-based confidence gate cannot certify unchanged old-task behavior within otherwise substantial budgets. A standard paired-binomial construction reduces this burden when outcome disagreements are rare. We also specify certified historical-reference promotion and a round-level missed-opportunity metric. In a constructed one-step pushing diagnostic with 32 seeds, fresh paired checks admit 31.6% of a common update stream at 2,000 episodes per stage, versus zero for the range-based gate; unconditional replay nevertheless learns better in closed-loop runs. A separate learned-dynamics stress test distinguishes model bias from feedback-selection error. The contribution is an admission-audit protocol with analytical and synthetic evidence; physical-robot and VLA validation remain open.

</details>


### [25] [Decoupling Readiness from Release for Tail-Aware Scheduling of Agentic LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10964)
*Bochao Feng,Jianjiang Li,Haojie Wang,Lin Qiao,Yinghui Li,Yukun Yan,Jidong Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种尾部风险感知的回合释放调度方法，通过均值-CVaR目标联合决定释放哪个就绪回合及维持多少未完成工作量，在竞争负载下显著降低工作流P95完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic LLM工作流由模型回合与工具交互交替组成，其端到端完成时间不仅取决于推理速度，还取决于就绪回合的释放时机。多数运行时采用"就绪即释放"的急切策略，在竞争负载下会积累已释放但未完成的工作，这些回合一旦提交便无法再被工作流级策略重排，从而加剧尾部延迟。

Method: 提出尾部风险感知的回合释放调度方法：1）联合决定下一个释放的就绪回合以及维持多少已释放但未完成的工作；2）采用均值-CVaR目标刻画未完成工作流的动态尾部风险；3）在优先级排序中引入回合工作量的在线估计；4）根据观测到的队列压力自适应调整已释放工作预算。

Result: 使用来自软件工程任务的真实智能体执行轨迹，在多种LLM与工作流到达率下进行评估。轻负载时性能与急切释放相当；在竞争负载下大幅降低工作流流时间的P95，最高实现3.50倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过尾部风险感知的释放调度，可有效缓解急切释放策略在竞争下的尾延迟问题，在不牺牲轻负载性能的前提下显著提升高负载下的完成时间表现。

Abstract: Agentic LLM workflows consist of sequences of model turns interleaved with tool interactions, so their end-to-end completion time depends not only on inference speed but also on when ready turns are released. Most runtimes release each turn immediately upon readiness. Under contention, this eager release policy can accumulate released but unfinished work; once submitted, those turns can no longer be reordered by the workflow-level policy, increasing tail latency. We present a tail-risk-aware turn release scheduling method that jointly decides which ready turn to release next and how much released but unfinished work to maintain. The method uses a mean--Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) objective to capture the evolving tail risk of unfinished workflows, incorporates online estimates of turn work when prioritizing ready turns, and adapts the released work budget to observed queue pressure. We evaluate the method using real agent execution traces from software engineering tasks across multiple LLMs and workflow arrival rates. The method performs comparably to eager release under light load and substantially reduces the P95 of workflow flow time under contention, achieving up to a \(3.50\times\) speedup.

</details>


### [26] [Demystifying the Privacy-Utility Trade-off in LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10992)
*Zhenhua Liu,Zhanxu Xie,Junjie Yu,Tong Zhu,Lijun Li,Wenliang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统分析隐私脱敏对下游性能的影响机制，提出意图驱动的本地保护框架，通过轻量模型Veilmind-4B实现动态提取-脱敏-恢复流程，在低泄漏隐私与高响应效用间取得更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型依赖上下文丰富的指令，导致敏感用户信息暴露；现有隐私保护方法多采用与上下文无关的静态规则，造成严重的效用下降，且脱敏如何影响下游性能的具体机制尚不明确。

Method: 开展系统性分析解构隐私-效用权衡，揭示三种机制：上下文相关效用（判断何时脱敏）、策略性适应（决定如何脱敏，即删除还是替换）、组合交互（扩展保护范围至属性间的语义依赖）。在此基础上提出意图驱动的本地保护框架，蒸馏轻量模型Veilmind-4B驱动动态的提取-脱敏-恢复流水线。

Result: 该方法在达到低泄漏隐私点的同时，保持了显著高于现有隐私导向基线的响应效用，将隐私-效用权衡推向帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 通过揭示隐私脱敏的三大底层机制并据此设计意图驱动的动态保护框架，可在保护用户隐私的同时大幅保留下游任务效用，优于传统静态规则方法。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models into daily tasks relies on context-rich instructions, inevitably exposing sensitive user information. Current privacy-preserving methods typically employ context-agnostic static rules, causing severe utility degradation. However, the specific mechanisms governing how sanitization impacts downstream performance remain largely underexplored. To address this, we conduct a systematic analysis to deconstruct the privacy-utility trade-off, uncovering three underlying mechanisms: (1) Context-Dependent Utility, which first establishes when to sanitize by revealing that data value shifts from critical constraints to dispensable noise based on user intent; (2) Strategic Adaptation, which subsequently determines how to sanitize by dictating that the choice between removal and replacement depends on the task's reliance on factual integrity versus structural coherence; and (3) Combinatorial Interplay, which finally extends the protection scope by demonstrating that attributes form a semantic web of synergistic dependencies or antagonistic redundancies. Guided by these insights, we introduce an intent-driven local protection framework. By distilling a lightweight model Veilmind-4B to drive a dynamic extraction-sanitization-restoration pipeline, our approach reaches a low-leakage privacy point while preserving substantially higher response utility than existing privacy-oriented baselines, advancing the privacy-utility trade-off toward the Pareto frontier.

</details>

*Mia Lassiter,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.AI

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI领域缺乏对"智能体"的统一界定，导致智能体研究在评估、比较和可复现性方面面临困难。作者希望通过系统性梳理来消除这一模糊性。

Method: 采用综述方法，将智能性分解为五个维度：环境交互、学习与适应、自主性、目标导向行为、时间一致性。对每个维度考察其在既有工作中的概念化方式，并综合归纳用于评估该维度的指标、基准与评估框架；同时构建并公开一个数字资源库（Agent Compendium）。

Result: 形成了关于智能体评估现状的结构化描述，明确了已有成熟方法以及评估仍存在局限或缺乏一致性的领域；并产出了可组织与扩展所识别评估方法的公开数字资源库Agent Compendium。

Conclusion: 综述与资源库共同为跨AI系统评估和比较智能体能力提供了统一结构，有助于提升研究的可复现性、沟通清晰度以及对人工智能体的系统化研究。

Abstract: The term agent in artificial intelligence lacks a standard definition, complicating the evaluation, comparison, and reproducibility of AI agent research. We address this ambiguity through a survey organized around five dimensions of agenticness: environmental interaction, learning and adaptation, autonomy, goal-directed behavior, and temporal coherence. For each dimension, we examine how the underlying capability has been conceptualized across prior work and synthesize the metrics, benchmarks, and evaluation frameworks used to assess it. This review provides a structured account of the current landscape of agent evaluation, highlighting both established approaches and areas where evaluation remains limited or inconsistent. We additionally introduce the Agent Compendium, a public-facing digital resource that organizes and extends the evaluation methods identified through this review. Together, the survey and compendium provide a common structure for evaluating and comparing agent capabilities across AI systems, supporting more reproducible research, clearer communication, and more systematic study of artificial agents.

</details>


### [28] [The Agent Incident Registry: Toward Preventing Repeated AI Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11030)
*Divyanshu Kumar,Rohith HN,Nitin Aravind Birur,Sahil Agarwal,Prashanth Harshangi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出智能体事件登记库AIR，汇编智能体相关公开事件，支持按来源检索案例并审计评估覆盖范围，而非估计失败率或控制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体越来越多通过工具和授权行动，但通用事件库难以捕捉将公开失败与智能体安全评测进行比较所需的机制信息，缺少可比对的公开事件与评测覆盖视角。

Method: 构建来源可追溯的AIR目录，含带支持证据、稳定标识符的记录，并对因果角色、披露类别、机制与结果采用缺失感知标注；由第二位人工评审复核全部记录及其标签；再以InjecAgent的案例集为参照开展部署类比审计，比较其与AIR覆盖面的差异。

Result: AIR中由智能体作为行动主体的生成系统记录里，部分涉及已实现危害，且结果类型与披露类别高度相关；已实现危害集中在真实场景与安全失败记录，负责任披露与研究演示多为“已演示”。InjecAgent案例仅覆盖AIR十二个表面中的三个，且全部为攻击者触发，而AIR还包含无对抗者的安全失败记录。

Conclusion: AIR可用于来源可溯的案例检索与评估范围审计，其聚合比例反映的是收录构成而非部署风险；该库不适用于失败率或控制有效性估计。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly act through tools and delegated authority, but general incident repositories rarely capture the mechanisms needed to compare public failures with agent-security evaluations. We present the Agent Incident Registry (AIR), a source-linked catalog containing \N{} records of agent-related events disclosed from \Yfirst{} through \Ylast{}. Each record includes supporting evidence, a stable identifier, and missingness-aware labels for causal role, disclosure class, mechanism, and outcome. Among the \Nprimary{} generative-system records in which the agent acted, \Rprimary{} involved realized harm (\Pprimary\%). Realized outcomes concentrate in in-the-wild and safety-failure records, while responsible disclosures and research demonstrations are overwhelmingly demonstrated; the aggregate share therefore characterizes collection composition rather than deployment risk. After initial curation, a second human reviewer checked all \N{} records and their existing labels for completeness and correctness. In a deployment-analogue audit, InjecAgent's \NInjecAgentCases{} cases occupy three of AIR's twelve surfaces and are all attacker-triggered, whereas AIR contains \Nsafety{} no-adversary safety failures. AIR supports source-grounded case retrieval and evaluation-scope auditing, not failure-rate or control-efficacy estimation.

</details>


### [29] [Grounding Agent Memory: Environment-Probing Curation for Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11060)
*Susheel Suresh,Hazel Mak,Sahil Bhatnagar,Chhaya Methani,Alejandro Gutierrez Munoz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"环境探测式记忆整理"（environment-probing curation），为异步记忆整理代理赋予只读世界工具，使其在部署时可核验、限定范围并刷新候选记忆，无需重训练、不改动任务代理与记忆表示，在GHCP与APEX任务上显著提升通过率并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆已进入生产级智能体平台，用于跨会话积累经验。但仅能访问已完成轨迹的事后记忆整理代理可能保留错误、过度泛化局部证据或保留过时知识，因此需要一种部署兼容的机制来校验与刷新记忆。

Method: 提出环境探测式整理：在现有异步整理代理基础上，赋予其最小权限的只读世界工具，用于检查、限定范围和刷新候选记忆。该方法不需要模型重训练，且不改变任务代理、检索器、记忆表示和生产写入权限。

Result: 在基于GHCP SDK的生产式测试环境中，对比无状态执行、全上下文学习、GHCP + Mem、GHCP + Mem（含环境探测）。在CLBench数据库探索任务上，探测将通过率从39%提升至73%，通过率折现奖励从8.60提升至22.60，同时每题查询数从8.8降至4.7，任务代理成本从3.38美元降至1.68美元。在六个APEX世界中，全部18个记忆对比基线的平均奖励比较均为正，任务代理工具调用下降16%–75%；探测在五个世界中取得最佳的任务代理单位美元奖励增益。在Sonnet 4.6和Opus 4.7上，探测的平均奖励均高于GHCP + Mem，且无模式漂移。

Conclusion: 环境探测使现有智能体记忆整理转变为环境知情、可审计的过程，同时保持紧凑的任务时接口。

Abstract: Persistent memory is entering production-oriented agent platforms to help long-horizon agents accumulate experience across sessions. Yet a post-task curator agent restricted to completed trajectories can preserve errors, overgeneralize partial evidence, or retain stale knowledge. We introduce environment-probing curation, a deployment-compatible extension that gives an existing asynchronous curator agent least-privilege, read-only world tools to check, scope, and refresh candidate memories. It requires no model retraining and leaves the task agent, retriever, memory representation, and production write authority unchanged. In a production-like GitHub Copilot (GHCP) harness built on its SDK, we compare stateless execution, full in-context learning, GHCP + Mem, and GHCP + Mem (w/ Env Probing) on CLBench database exploration and 90 adapted APEX management-consulting tasks. On CLBench, probing raises pass rate from 39% to 73% and pass-discounted reward from 8.60 to 22.60 while reducing queries from 8.8 to 4.7 per question and task-agent cost from \$3.38 to \$1.68. Across six APEX worlds, all 18 memory-versus-baseline mean reward comparisons are positive and task-agent tool calls fall by 16--75%; probing gives the best task-agent reward gain per dollar in five worlds. Probing also attains higher mean reward than GHCP + Mem on both Sonnet 4.6 and Opus 4.7 without schema drift. Environment probing therefore turns existing agent-memory curation into an environment-informed, auditable process while preserving a compact task-time interface.

</details>


### [30] [A Voice-Interactive Multi-Agent System for Smart Operating Rooms: Architecture Design and Key Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11231)
*Tianxiang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SurgicalRoomAgent，一个基于大语言模型的语音交互多智能体手术室系统，采用分层架构实现语音理解、设备控制、术中记录与报告生成，并研究KV Cache前缀预热、流式JSON解析、渐进式技能提示三项关键技术，实验证明其在16,384 token上下文内满足手术室实时性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术室场景对实时性、多设备协同与低延迟交互有严格要求，而现有LLM推理与上下文限制难以直接满足。本文旨在构建面向智能手术室的语音交互多智能体系统，解决推理延迟高、端到端响应慢、上下文窗口受限等痛点，使LLM能在手术室实时环境中稳定运行。

Method: 系统采用分层架构：语音交互流水线（唤醒、ASR、轮次检测、智能体推理、TTS）与智能体核心（技能注册表、任务规划器、设备管理器）。关键技术包括：(1) KV Cache前缀预热，基于字节级最长公共前缀复用，将重计算开销从约500ms降至数十毫秒；(2) 流式部分JSON解析与提前并行任务执行，端到端延迟降低约30%；(3) 渐进式技能提示披露，依据用户角色、已连设备与手术阶段动态筛选系统提示以提升上下文信息密度。系统使用Qwen3-27B模型与llama.cpp/sglang推理引擎实现。

Result: 实验分析表明，系统可在16,384 token上下文限制内有效运行，多设备并行控制响应时间满足手术室实时要求。

Conclusion: SurgicalRoomAgent通过分层架构与三项关键优化技术，验证了LLM驱动的语音交互多智能体系统在智能手术室中的可行性与实时性，为手术室人机协同与设备控制提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This paper presents SurgicalRoomAgent, a voice-interactive multi-agent system for smart operating rooms based on large language models (LLMs). The system achieves natural language understanding, device control, intraoperative recording, and surgical report generation through a layered architecture comprising a voice interaction pipeline (wake, ASR, turn detection, agent reasoning, TTS) and an agent core (skill registry, task planner, device manager). Three key technologies are investigated: (1) KV Cache prefix warming for low-latency inference, reducing recomputation overhead from approximately 500 ms to tens of milliseconds via byte-level Longest Common Prefix reuse; (2) streaming partial JSON parsing with early parallel task execution, reducing end-to-end latency by approximately 30%; and (3) progressive skill prompt disclosure, which dynamically filters system prompts based on user role, connected devices, and surgical phase to maximize information density within limited context windows. The system is implemented using the Qwen3-27B model with llama.cpp/sglang inference engines. Experimental analysis demonstrates effective operation within a 16,384-token context limit and multi-device parallel control response times meeting OR real-time requirements.

</details>


### [31] [Fork Where the Model Changes Its Mind: Belief-Shift Branching for Tree-Structured Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11061)
*Bin Lei,Yu Li,Prafulla Kumar Choubey,Jiaxin Zhang,Becky Xiangyu Peng,Qinyuan Ye,Kartik Narayan,Caiwen Ding,Silvio Savarese,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"信念偏移分支"（belief-shift branching），将树状 rollout 的分叉点放置问题形式化为定位价值曲线枢轴，通过读取模型答案信念信号在最不确定处开叉，在无步骤级监督下提升了可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）的步骤级信用分配效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树状 rollout 能为无评论家的 RLVR 提供步骤级信用，但每个分叉都增加采样成本，实际预算仅允许每条链少数几个分叉。若分叉点选在结果已基本确定的步骤，兄弟分支结论趋同，几乎不提供信用信号。因此固定树规模下，分叉位置决定步骤级 RL 的收益。现有主流方法按结构（固定长度、中点、分隔符）或下一 token 熵来放置分叉，未必落在结果真正转折处。

Method: 将分叉放置形式化为定位链价值曲线的"枢轴"（期望结果发生转折之处）。提出信念偏移分支：在候选边界读取模型答案信念，在相邻信念差异最大的步骤之前开叉。给出三种无需步骤级监督的实例，覆盖不同访问层级：黑盒探测、logit-lens 深度剖面、离线拟合的激活方向（仅在 RL 训练前验证中使用）。该信号仅用于放置分叉，探测开销在数学上约占步骤计算的 1%，代码上低于 5%。

Result: 在验证中，与蒙特卡洛价值曲线对比，信念偏移信号在全部八个"模型×基准"面板中排名第一，优于熵、结构和 LLM 评判基线。在三个模型家族、两个领域的 RL 中，信念偏移分叉在数学聚合指标上全面领先，在 OLMo-3-7B 上较最强基线提升 +2.6 聚合分，AIME 2026 上 +2.9；并横扫所有 OLMo 代码列，LiveCodeBench-medium 提升 +6.5。

Conclusion: 分叉位置是步骤级 RLVR 收益的关键杠杆，而信念偏移是放置分叉的有效信号，在低成本、无步骤级监督条件下显著优于结构、熵及 LLM 评判方法，并在实际 RL 训练中带来稳定的数学与代码性能提升。

Abstract: Tree-structured rollouts give critic-free reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) step-level credit: fork a chain at an intermediate point, and sibling outcome differences estimate step value. Each fork adds sampling cost, so realistic budgets typically allow only a few forks per chain. A fork placed where the outcome is already largely settled yields siblings that mostly agree and provide almost no credit signal; hence, for a given tree size, where forks are placed largely determines how much step-level RL can gain. Most existing mainstream methods place forks by structure, such as fixed lengths, midpoints, and delimiters, or by next-token entropy. We formalize fork placement as locating the \emph{pivots} of the chain's value curve, where the expected outcome turns. We propose \emph{belief-shift branching}: read the model's answer belief at candidate boundaries and fork just before the step where consecutive beliefs diverge most. Three instantiations, none needing step-level supervision, span access levels: a black-box probe, a logit-lens depth profile, and a learned activation direction, which is fit offline and therefore used only in the validation before RL training. The signal only \emph{places} forks, and the probe costs about $1\%$ of step compute on mathematics and under $5\%$ on code when it runs inside the rollout engine. In that validation, against Monte-Carlo value curves, a belief-shift signal ranks first in each of the eight model$\times$benchmark panels, ahead of entropy, structural, and LLM-judge baselines. In RL across three model families and two domains, belief-shift forking leads every mathematics aggregate, on OLMo-3-7B by $+2.6$ aggregate and $+2.9$ on AIME 2026 over the strongest baseline, and sweeps every OLMo code column, by $+6.5$ on LiveCodeBench-medium.

</details>


### [32] [The Convention Gap: Towards Measuring Implicit Communication in Cooperative AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11489)
*Makoto Fukushima,Hua-Dong Xiong,Ehsan Moradi Pari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"约定差距"（convention gap）指标，通过Hanabi游戏的人-人、AI-AI、人-AI对局数据，量化隐含交流约定，发现人类对局存在显著约定差距而AI-AI几乎为零，且该指标比游戏得分更能预测AI与人类合作的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作型AI通常仅与其他AI对局来评估，但人类合作依赖隐含约定（超越字面信息的共享协议），这类约定可能无法被AI-AI基准捕捉到，因此需要一个能衡量隐含交流的量化指标。

Method: 提出"约定差距"指标，即依据交流字面内容预测的失败概率与实际观测失败率之差。在Hanabi中，有限的牌堆和确定性提示约束使这一后验可精确计算。研究者回放了约101,000个游戏动作，来自三个人类-人类（hanab.live）、AI-AI（HOAD）和人-AI（HanabiData）公开数据集；并以Off-Belief Learning智能体（约定内容可控）做已知答案验证。

Result: 约定差距在人类配对中为+26.2个百分点，AI配对为−0.7pp，人-AI配对为+16.4pp，且集中在未收到提示的牌的打法上（人类配对中达+46pp）。在人-AI对局中，人类可用的字面信息在三个AI伙伴间相近（预测失败率38–41%），但人类失败率在14.4%–34.4%间波动，差距在+24.1到+6.2pp之间；引发最大差距的伙伴反而导致最少的人类失败。游戏得分依赖于各语料库的成员构成，而约定差距能在智能体层面区分人类与AI对局。Off-Belief Learning智能体在无约定时为+1.6pp，并单调上升至+21.7pp。

Conclusion: 结果表明，约定兼容性而非AI-AI表现，可能才是预测AI与人类伙伴合作有效性的关键因素。

Abstract: Cooperative AI agents are evaluated against other AIs, yet human cooperation relies on implicit conventions---shared protocols for reading meaning beyond the literal message---which AI-AI benchmarks may not capture. We propose the \emph{convention gap}, the difference between the failure probability predicted from the literal content of communication and the observed failure rate, as a metric of implicit communication. In the card game Hanabi, the finite deck and deterministic hint constraints make this posterior exactly computable. We replayed about 101,000 play actions from three public datasets of human-human (hanab.live), AI-AI (HOAD), and human-AI (HanabiData) games. The gap was +26.2 percentage points (pp) in human pairs, $-$0.7~pp in AI pairs, and +16.4~pp in human-AI pairs, and was concentrated on plays of cards that had received no hints (+46~pp in human pairs). Within human-AI play, the literal information available to humans was similar across the three AI partners (mean predicted failure 38--41\%), but human failure rates ranged from 14.4\% to 34.4\% and the gap from +24.1 to +6.2~pp; the partner eliciting the largest gap produced the fewest human failures. Game score carried different information: it depended on each corpus's roster composition, whereas the gap separated human from AI play at the agent level. As a known-answer check, Off-Belief Learning agents, whose convention content is controlled by construction, gave a gap of +1.6~pp at the convention-free level, rising monotonically to +21.7~pp. These results suggest that convention compatibility, rather than AI-AI performance, may predict an AI's effectiveness with human partners.

</details>


### [33] [MOSAIC: Query-Aware Exploration Policy Adaptation for GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11065)
*EunKyeong Lee,Kyeong-Jin Oh,Jinwon Kim,Hye Woo Lee,Minsang Song,Hyeongjun Jang,Junyoung Youn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mosaic将GraphRAG检索重构为逐查询的控制问题，通过LLM分析器为每个查询生成有界的检索策略，在多个基准上显著超越已有方法且更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphRAG系统大多对所有查询采用基本共享的探索过程，但不同查询存在结构性错配：直接事实需要紧凑的局部邻域，对比问题需要多个目标的均衡覆盖，而中介性问题可能需要穿越弱相关连接器的更深路径。固定策略无法普遍最优。

Method: 提出免训练的Mosaic框架，将GraphRAG检索形式化为逐查询的控制问题。由LLM分析器把查询特定的证据需求转化为对种子选择、图遍历、停止条件和证据选择的有界策略；而语料图、索引、评分函数、接地过程和答案生成器保持共享。

Result: 在GraphRAG-Bench上，Mosaic在Medical上取得76.97的查询加权答案正确率、在Novel上取得64.33，分别比最强已报告总体结果提升5.13和4.43分；在Medical上证据召回率达95.1、上下文相关性86.1。同一图与生成器的受控对比显示，没有固定窄、中、宽策略能持续最优，Mosaic比最强固定策略提升9.96分；相对Fixed Wide，评估路径减少81.9%、保留证据项减少47.2%。在HotpotQA、MuSiQue和2WikiMultiHopQA上的迁移实验表明该策略接口无需基准特定检索器训练即可应用。

Conclusion: 逐查询自适应的检索控制在准确率与效率上均优于固定探索策略，且具备良好的跨数据集迁移能力，验证了将GraphRAG检索视为控制问题的有效性。

Abstract: Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) can connect evidence distributed across a corpus graph, but most systems use largely shared exploration procedures across queries. This creates a structural mismatch: direct facts may need compact local neighborhoods, comparisons need balanced coverage of multiple targets, and mediated questions may require deeper paths through weakly related connectors. We present Mosaic, a training-free framework that formulates GraphRAG retrieval as a per-query control problem. An LLM analyzer converts query-specific evidence requirements into a bounded policy over seed selection, graph traversal, stopping, and evidence selection, while the corpus graph, indexes, scoring functions, grounding procedure, and answer generator remain shared.
  On GraphRAG-Bench, Mosaic achieves query-weighted Answer Correctness of 76.97 on Medical and 64.33 on Novel, improving over the strongest previously reported overall results by 5.13 and 4.43 points. On Medical, it reaches 95.1 Evidence Recall and 86.1 Context Relevancy. Controlled comparisons on an identical graph and generator show that no fixed narrow, medium, or wide policy is consistently optimal; Mosaic improves by 9.96 points over the strongest canonical fixed policy. Relative to Fixed Wide, it evaluates 81.9% fewer paths and retains 47.2% fewer evidence items. Transfer experiments on HotpotQA, MuSiQue, and 2WikiMultiHopQA further show that the policy interface can be applied without benchmark-specific retriever training.

</details>


### [34] [Benchmark Radar: A Living Database and Search Engine for AI Benchmarks and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11115)
*Koutian Wu,Junjie Zhou,Ergan Shang,Jiayu Wang,Pengqian Han,Junkai Wang,Wanghan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Benchmark Radar，一个面向 AI 基准测试（benchmark）的“活”数据库与搜索引擎，支持检索、发现与证据追踪，并发布网页仪表盘、排行榜、CLI 及可复现分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型与 AI 系统的研究者及开发者需要找到相关评测、定位其基准数据集与代码，并理解分数背后的设置，但现有信息分散、缺乏统一检索与证据追溯手段。

Method: 系统结合每日发现（37 个来源：13 个直连器与 24 个一方研究与工程源）与可搜索的基准目录，收录模型卡与技术报告中的提及及分数历史；保留来源身份与引用；构建包含 1,283 条源记录（来自 4 个基准目录）与 12,916 条数值观测（覆盖 790 条记录）的目录，并提供检索与审计。

Result: 对完整目录进行了审计，分析了基准饱和、采用趋势与分数比较的局限；通过一个完整的前人工作检索示例，展示如何查询目录并核查基准证据。

Conclusion: 发布网页仪表盘（含排行榜、分数与实测使用量的帕累托前沿视图、饱和与趋势视图、每日信息流、可下载证据）、离线查询 CLI 及可复现分析，为基准检索与发现提供统一平台。

Abstract: Benchmark researchers and developers of large language models (LLMs) and other AI systems need to find relevant evaluations, locate their benchmark datasets and code, and understand the settings behind reported scores. We present Benchmark Radar, a living database and search engine for retrieval and discovery of AI benchmarks, covering LLM evaluation, agentic and tool-use benchmarks, coding, reasoning, safety, and domain-specific evaluations. The system combines daily discovery of benchmark papers, repositories, datasets, and releases with a searchable benchmark catalog, mentions in model cards and technical reports, and score histories. It retains source identities and citations so readers can inspect candidate benchmarks and their evaluation evidence. Daily discovery draws on 37 sources: 13 direct connectors and 24 first-party research and engineering feeds. The catalog contains 1,283 source records drawn from 4 benchmark catalogs and 12,916 numeric observations on 790 records. We describe collection and retrieval, audit the full catalog, and examine benchmark saturation, adoption trends, and the limits of score comparisons. A worked example walks through a complete prior-art search, showing how to query the catalog and inspect benchmark evidence when designing a new evaluation. We release the web dashboard with a benchmark leaderboard, a Pareto frontier view of score against measured use, saturation and trend views, daily feeds, downloadable evidence, a command-line interface (CLI) for offline queries, and reproducible analysis.

</details>


### [35] [KuaiRP Series Role-playing Models Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11127)
*Yipeng Wang,Ziwei Zhang,Jiahui Zhang,Qi Gan,Kai Sheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KuaiRP角色扮演模型系列完整技术方案，通过多阶段训练（SFT数据流水线、规则奖励RL、两阶段在线策略蒸馏）在注入深度领域知识的同时恢复通用智能体能力，实现低成本和高质量部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向专用角色扮演模型提出四个核心目标：简化提示工程、输出质量高度稳定、内置领域世界知识、小参数高效部署。但注入深度领域知识会导致通用智能体能力严重灾难性遗忘，需克服这一权衡问题。

Method: 多阶段训练流程：(1) 设计标准化角色模板，基于用户行为模拟与反向画像过滤构建SFT数据流水线；(2) RL阶段采用基于规则的复合奖励函数，消除长度膨胀与重复生成等退化现象；(3) 提出带累积散度衰减(CDD)的两阶段在线策略蒸馏(OPD)自蒸馏范式，以领域适配模型为教师、原始基座模型为学生，恢复通用能力。

Result: 实验表明KuaiRP模型在目标领域内角色扮演保真度可媲美当前最先进的闭源模型，同时成功恢复通用智能体能力，并保持极低的部署成本。

Conclusion: 多阶段训练加自蒸馏范式有效平衡了深度领域知识注入与通用智能体能力保持之间的权衡，为低成本、高质量的角色扮演模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces the complete technical solution for the KuaiRP series of role-playing models. We aim to achieve four core objectives for a dedicated role-playing model: simplified prompt engineering, highly stable output quality, built-in domain world knowledge, and high-efficiency deployment with a small parameter size. However, effectively injecting deep domain knowledge often leads to a severe catastrophic forgetting of the model's general agent capabilities. To overcome this trade-off, we propose a multi-stage training pipeline. First, we design a standardized character template and construct an SFT data pipeline based on user behavior simulation and reverse profile filtering. Next, we utilize a rule-based composite reward function during the Reinforcement Learning (RL) phase to eliminate common degradation phenomena like length expansion and repetitive generation. Finally, to recover the general capabilities compromised during SFT and RL, we propose a novel self-distillation paradigm using Two-stage On-Policy Distillation (OPD) equipped with Cumulative-Divergence Decay (CDD). By using the domain-adapted model as the teacher and the original base model as the student, we effectively balance deep domain knowledge injection with the preservation of general agent capabilities. Experimental results demonstrate that the KuaiRP models not only match the current state-of-the-art proprietary models in role-playing fidelity within our target domains, but also successfully recover general agent capabilities, maintaining extremely low deployment costs.

</details>


### [36] [Same Day, Same Story; One Day Ahead, a Different Signal: The Dual Validity of Financial Sentiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11144)
*AS Aravinthkakshan,Laven Srivastava,Harsh Nandwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 金融NLP常规流程假定"与人工标注的一致性"等同于"预测市场信号的能力"，本文在同一样本上同时测量二者，发现这一假设并不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融NLP的标准工作流是先用人工标签验证情感工具，再信任其提取市场信号。然而这两种评估是否测量同一件事从未被检验，作者希望在能同时测得两者的场景中检验该假设。

Method: 构建2002–2025年证券集体诉讼语料，将70,500条X消息与异常股票收益关联，并配有人工标注金标样本。用同一流程运行五种工具（VADER、Loughran-McDonald、FinBERT、Twitter-RoBERTa及一个LLM标注器），比较构造效度与预测效度在不同抽样约定和分数表示下的关系。

Result: 传统按方法特定抽样时，人工一致性更贴近同日分级关联而非滞后一日关联；在固定样本面板上，一致性在两个时间窗的分级秩相关相似，但粗粒度排序仍弱。语料中17.6%为垃圾信息，消息量既不能预测市场损失也不能预测和解金额。

Conclusion: 基准一致性只能确立语义效度，本身无法决定预测排序；构造效度与预测效度的关系取决于抽样约定与分数表示。垃圾信息占比高时消息量不具预测力。

Abstract: Financial NLP has a standard workflow: validate a sentiment tool against human labels, then trust it to extract market signal. This assumes the two evaluations measure the same thing. We test that assumption in a setting where both can be measured at once: a corpus of securities class actions (2002-2025) linking 70,500 X messages to abnormal stock returns, with a single-annotator human labelled gold sample. Running five instruments (VADER, Loughran-McDonald, FinBERT, Twitter-RoBERTa, and an LLM annotator) through one identical pipeline, we find that the relationship between construct and predictive validity depends on the sampling convention and score representation. Under conventional method-specific sampling, human agreement aligns more closely with graded same-day associations than with one-day leads. On a fixed-n panel, however, agreement has similar graded rank correlations at both horizons, while the coarse ordering remains weak. Benchmark agreement therefore establishes semantic validity but does not by itself determine predictive rankings. In a conversation that is 17.6% spam, message volume predicts neither market damage nor settlement size.

</details>


### [37] [The Oligarch Barely Steers Model Collapse in Multi-Model Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11146)
*Yangze Liu,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在受控的多模型生态中，市场集中度几乎不改变模型崩塌的速度与终点；崩塌节奏主要由"谁在提供训练池文本"及其被同化的难易度决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成文本回流进下一代模型训练语料，递归训练会引发模型崩塌。已有研究多假设市场均分，但现实中的生成式AI呈寡头格局。作者担心集中化带来两个后果：更少更同质的来源可能加速崩塌，以及后来的模型可能被拖向寡头输出。本文旨在检验这两种担忧。

Method: 构建受控生态系统：13个1–4B开源模型形成3至13个参与者的自然生态，并注入一个探针将最高份额推至90%。每一代中，各模型输出按市场份额混入共享池，每个模型都从干净基座权重在该池上重训，共进行五代。通过改变份额与身份、替换生态成员、构造份额加权的易感度指数，以及用人类文本替换一半池内容来做对照实验，共考察十九个实验臂。

Result: 两种担忧在测试范围内均未出现，取而代之的是一种不变性：份额越不均，崩塌速度几乎不变；终点移动更小，份额与身份旋钮对五代终点的改变仅为所有实验臂共同漂移量的百分之几。极端份额叠加最强注入偏置仍不足以保证操控，产生的主题偏移在崩塌度量上也只留微弱痕迹。真正决定速度的是供应方身份及其被带动程度：份额固定时，替换K=3生态的成员使五代漂移变化达2.8倍；份额加权的易感度指数以R²=0.68解释十九个实验臂的速度差异；用人类文本替换一半池内容约使漂移减半而不改变其走向。

Conclusion: 在测试范围内，集中度既不决定模型崩塌的终点，也不决定其速度；崩塌的节奏跟随的是"谁的文本填满了训练池"。研究提示关注点应从市场份额的分配转向参与者的身份与易感性。

Abstract: AI-generated text is flowing back into the training corpora of the next generation of models. Recursive training on it drives model collapse, and recent work extends the setting to many models feeding one another -- but almost always with the market split evenly, while real generative AI is an oligopoly. Concentration raises two worries: fewer, more uniform sources may make collapse faster, and later models may be dragged toward the oligarch's output. We test both in controlled ecosystems: 13 open 1--4B models form natural ecosystems of 3 to 13 players, plus an injected probe that pushes the top share to 90%; each generation, every model's output is mixed into a shared pool by market share and every model is retrained on that pool from clean base weights, for five generations. Yet within the range we test, neither worry materializes; what emerges instead is an invariance. Making the split more unequal barely changes the speed of collapse. Destinations move even less: the share and identity knobs shift five-generation endpoints by only a few percent of the drift common to all arms -- the ecosystems collapse to nearly the same place.
