  models, ActMap consistently outperforms sampling, token-probability, attention,
  and embedding baselines, and matches ACT-ViT, a detector trained on dense
  activation tensors $67 \times$ larger, at essentially the same mean AUROC with
  lower calibration error on ten of twelve pairs. The resulting score supports
  abstention, routing, and selective verification from a single generation, making
  it a practical primitive for scalable oversight of deployed models.

</details>


### [72] [Extending SMT Solving with Non-Ground Clause Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11509)
*Yasmine Briefs,Christoph Weidenbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种演算，结合地面实例化、CDCL(T)风格规则与非地面冲突分析，使冲突分析直接作用在原始非地面子句上，从而学到更一般、甚至非冗余的子句，并可统一模拟CDCL、SCL(FOL)、SCL(T)乃至Resolution。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前非地面SMT求解主要依赖量词实例化：求解器生成地面实例并用CDCL(T)风格推理求解。但冲突分析只学习地面子句，忽略了冲突来源于非地面子句实例这一事实。而非地面推理可比纯地面推理产生指数级更短的证明，因此有必要引入非地面推理能力。

Method: 提出一个演算，由地面实例化、CDCL(T)风格规则和非地面冲突分析组成。求解器仍在地面实例上推理，但冲突分析中的归结步骤在原始非地面子句上执行，从而产生比地面冲突更一般的学习子句。配合适当策略，学习子句甚至是非冗余的。此外还展示了如何将时序回溯（chronological backtracking）纳入SMT求解。

Result: 该演算产生通常比地面冲突更一般的学习子句；在合适策略下学习子句是非冗余的；并成功纳入时序回溯。该演算为CDCL(T)风格SMT求解、一系列基于实例化的过程以及非地面子句学习提供了统一框架，并证明其可模拟CDCL、SCL(FOL)、SCL(T)甚至Resolution。

Conclusion: 非地面冲突分析能够生成更一般的学习子句并保持非冗余性，时序回溯也可被纳入SMT求解。所提演算统一了多种SMT求解范式，并具备模拟CDCL、SCL(FOL)、SCL(T)和Resolution的表达能力。

Abstract: Quantifier instantiation is currently the main approach to non-ground SMT solving: solvers generate ground instances and solve the resulting ground SMT problems with CDCL(T)-style reasoning. When a conflict is found, conflict analysis learns only a ground clause, even though the conflict comes from instances of non-ground clauses. Yet non-ground reasoning can give exponentially shorter proofs than purely ground reasoning. We propose a calculus that consists of ground instantiations, CDCL(T)-style rules, and non-ground conflict analysis. The solver reasons on ground instances, but the resolution steps of conflict analysis are performed on their original non-ground clauses. This produces learned clauses that are typically more general than the ground conflict. With a suitable strategy, the learned clauses are even non-redundant. We also show how chronological backtracking can be included in SMT solving. Our calculus gives a common setting for CDCL(T)-style SMT solving, a range of instantiation-based procedures, and non-ground clause learning, and we prove that it simulates CDCL, SCL(FOL), SCL(T), and even Resolution.

</details>


### [73] [Lightweight LiDAR-Based Cone Detection Framework Using Random Forest for Formula Student Driverless](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11527)
*Márk Mező-Kerekes,Péter Praksz,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种面向Formula Student Driverless纯CPU执行的轻量级激光雷达感知流水线，结合地面去除、IMU运动补偿、DBSCAN聚类与基于几何特征的随机森林分类，在2371个真实标注聚类上取得98.33% F1分数、端到端3.13毫秒CPU推理时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Formula Student Driverless车辆需要可靠、低延迟的感知能力，但现有很多流水线依赖深度学习与多传感器融合，通常需要GPU加速，在资源受限的赛车上难以部署。作者希望提供一种不依赖深度学习和多传感器融合、可在纯CPU上运行的轻量级感知方案。

Method: 构建仅使用激光雷达的感知流水线，包含地面去除、基于IMU的运动补偿、DBSCAN聚类，以及基于几何特征的随机森林分类；并通过特征重要性分析将模型输入从12个特征降至7个，同时保持性能。

Result: 在来自真实FSD赛事的2371个标注聚类上评估，流水线达到98.33%的F1分数，纯CPU硬件上端到端运行时间为3.13毫秒。

Conclusion: 该研究证明无需GPU的轻量级LiDAR-only方案可实现高精度、低延迟感知；作者公开数据集、标注工具与训练模型，为其他资源受限的自动驾驶赛车队提供实用且可复现的基线。

Abstract: Reliable, low-latency perception is crucial for Formula Student Driverless vehicles, yet many existing pipelines rely on deep learning and multi-sensor fusion, often requiring GPU acceleration. This paper presents a lightweight LiDAR-only perception pipeline tailored for CPU execution, combining ground removal, IMU-based motion compensation, DBSCAN clustering, and geometric feature-based Random Forest classification. Feature importance analysis reduced the model input from 12 to 7 features while preserving performance. Evaluated on 2,371 labeled clusters collected from real FSD events, the pipeline achieves an F1-score of 98.33% and an end-to-end runtime of 3.13 ms on CPU-only hardware. The released dataset, labeling tool, and trained models provide a practical and reproducible baseline for other resource-constrained autonomous racing teams.

</details>


### [74] [Prompt Revision as a Source of Cultural Bias in Text-to-Image Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11532)
*Aleksandra Urman,Elsa Lichtenegger,Salima Jaoua,Azza Bouleimen,Robin Forsberg,Corinna Hertweck,Stefania Ionescu,Nicolò Pagan,Ancsa Hannak,Joachim Baumann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出WORLDVIEW基准（15种语言、31个语言-文化配对、8,960条提示词），首次审计商业文生图系统的提示词改写层，发现改写层本身是非西方文化刻板印象的因果来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业文生图系统会在用户不可见、不可关闭的情况下静默改写提示词，但现有文化偏见审计只考察最终图像、把生成视为单一流程，因而无法定位偏见来源。作者希望把审计对象从"模型"扩展到"实际部署的系统"。

Method: 构建多语言基准WORLDVIEW（8,960条提示词、15种语言、31个语言-文化配对），对DALL-E-3、Imagen-4、GPT-Image-1.5三个系统的改写层做三步分析：(1)对每种文化语境标记的强度；(2)是否将该语境压缩为狭窄词汇；(3)该词汇是否刻板。并以无上下文的英文提示为基线，对比原始提示与改写提示在无改写层模型上生成的图像。

Result: 相对于无上下文英文基线，美国是被标记最少的情境；非西方、非英语情境被标记程度高得多，被压缩为跨主题反复套用的狭窄词汇，并被简化为可识别的文化刻板印象。对无改写层模型比较原始提示与改写提示的图像，可确认改写层本身是这种刻板化的因果来源。

Conclusion: 要定位并修复文化偏见，必须审计实际部署的系统而非孤立的模型；改写层是一个此前未被记录、且具有因果作用的偏见来源。

Abstract: Commercial text-to-image systems silently revise user prompts before generating images, a step users typically cannot disable or even see. Yet, existing audits of cultural bias examine only the final images and treat generation as a single pipeline, so they cannot tell where the bias originates. We introduce WORLDVIEW, a multilingual benchmark of 8,960 prompts across 15 languages and 31 language-context pairings. Using it, we audit the revision layer in three systems (DALL-E-3, Imagen-4, GPT-Image-1.5) through a three-step analysis of how heavily it marks each cultural context, whether it flattens that context into a narrow vocabulary, and whether that vocabulary is stereotypical. Relative to a no-context English baseline, the US is the least-marked context, while non-Western and non-Anglophone contexts are marked far more heavily, flattened into narrow vocabularies applied across topically diverse prompts, and reduced to recognizable cultural stereotypes. Comparing images from original versus revised prompts on models without a revision layer, we identify the layer itself as a previously undocumented, causal source of this stereotyping. To locate cultural bias, and fix it, we must audit the system as deployed, not the model alone.

</details>


### [75] [Characterizing Job Power Elasticity for Power-Flexible AI Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11542)
*Philip Colangelo,Charles Dawson,Shayan Sengupta,Ayse Coskun,Varun Sivaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统刻画了LLM训练的"作业功率弹性"，提出功率弹性指数（PFI）度量降功率的性能代价，并证明PFI感知的功率分配可在功率约束下最大化吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM训练是数据中心增长最快的电力需求来源之一，电力供给已成为AI基础设施扩张的主要瓶颈。若能使训练负载的功耗具备弹性，可为AI增长释放额外电力、抑制电价上涨并提升现有电网利用率。但要实现这种弹性，必须先理解GPU功率降低时训练负载性能如何变化。

Method: 论文对LLM训练中的"作业功率弹性"（吞吐对降功率的敏感度）进行了首次系统刻画；提出归一化指标"功率弹性指数（PFI）"来量化降功率的性能代价，并作为支持SLA感知的功率弹性控制原语。数据来自131次LLM训练运行（H200平台，另有24次H200验证运行与34次匹配的H100运行），涵盖稠密与MoE模型、预训练与微调任务，规模最多32块GPU；同时识别可在运行时预测PFI的遥测信号。

Result: LLM训练作业表现出显著但差异化的功率弹性；所识别的遥测信号可在运行时预测PFI；PFI感知的功率分配能在功率约束下最大化总tokens/秒吞吐。在30%降功率条件下，PFI感知分配每作业可恢复约1.5k tokens/s，达到等权分配与拥有完美信息的神谕（oracle）之间性能差距的63%。

Conclusion: 研究确立功率弹性为训练作业可度量的属性，为功率感知、可响应电网的AI基础设施奠定基础。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) training is among the fastest-growing sources of electricity demand in modern data centers, and power availability is a primary bottleneck to continued AI infrastructure growth. Making the power consumption of these workloads flexible could unlock additional power for AI growth, limit increases in electricity prices, and improve the utilization of existing grid infrastructure. However, to realize this flexibility, we must first understand how the performance of training workloads changes when GPU power is reduced.
  This paper presents the first systematic characterization of \emph{job power elasticity} (the sensitivity of throughput to power reductions) in LLM training. To quantify elasticity, we introduce the \emph{Power Flexibility Index (PFI)}, a normalized metric that quantifies the performance cost of power reductions and provides a control primitive for SLA-aware power flexibility.
  We collect data from 131 LLM training runs on H200 (plus 24 H200 validation runs and 34 matched H100 runs), including both dense and mixture-of-experts models, pretraining and fine-tuning tasks, and up to 32 GPUs. We find that LLM training jobs exhibit substantial but variable power elasticity, and we identify telemetry signals that predict PFI at runtime. Finally, we demonstrate that PFI-aware power allocation maximizes total tokens/second throughput under power constraints. Under a 30\% power reduction, PFI-aware power allocation recovers ~1.5k tokens/s per job, 63\% of the performance gap between an equal-weight allocation and an oracle with perfect information. Our results establish power elasticity as a measurable property of training jobs and provide a foundation for power-aware, grid-responsive AI infrastructure.

</details>


### [76] [Enabling Knowledge Graph Understanding at Scale with the EXplore Your Graphs ENgine (EXYGEN)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11569)
*Harshdeep Singh,Yurui Zhu,Giovanni Colavizza,Matteo Romanello*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 EXYGEN 框架，实现大规模知识图谱的对话式访问。研究分两步：(1) 在仅使用自动派生结构化元数据和小样本的情况下评估 LLM 的 text-to-SPARQL 生成能力；(2) 为超大规模 KG 设计谓词覆盖感知的并行图采样策略以生成所需元数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现大规模知识图谱的对话式访问面临两大挑战：一是能否在不进行任务特定微调的情况下，仅依赖自动派生的结构化元数据和小样本让 LLM 完成 text-to-SPARQL 生成；二是该方法所依赖的元数据在超大规模 KG 上生成计算上不可行。论文依次解决这两个问题。

Method: (1) 将 VoID 描述和 ShEx 模式集成到 RAG 管道中，并在 SciQA 基准上对 KG 派生上下文进行消融；最佳配置结合 ShEx 模式、检索三元组和示例问答对。(2) 提出谓词覆盖感知的并行图采样策略，在保持结构多样性的同时保证计算可行性，并在 OpenCitations Meta、GESIS 和 ORKG 上验证。

Result: 最佳配置在无需 LLM 微调的情况下，执行结果精确匹配达到 0.419。词汇类指标（如 F1）难以预测查询正确性；在上下文充足时，大型通用 LLM 可超越小型代码专用模型。采样策略在 OpenCitations Meta 和 GESIS 上保持高谓词覆盖且三元组损失极小，运行时间减少 80 倍以上；在 ORKG 上采样不仅是更快路径，更是获得完整元数据的唯一可行途径。

Conclusion: 结构化模式上下文与轻量级提示可显著减少对微调的依赖，从而支持可扩展的 KG 对话式访问；但要缩小与完全微调方法的差距，可能需要减少对人工标注问答示例的依赖（如通过合成生成或执行反馈驱动的方法），并在单一基准之外验证这些发现。

Abstract: We present EXYGEN (EXplore Your Graphs ENgine), a framework for knowledge graph (KG) understanding that enables conversational access to KGs at scale. We address two questions in sequence. First, how effectively can LLMs perform text-to-SPARQL generation given only automatically derived structured metadata and small graph samples, rather than task-specific fine-tuning? We integrate VoID descriptions and ShEx schemas into a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline and ablate KG-derived context on the SciQA benchmark. Our best configuration -- combining ShEx schemas, retrieved triples, and example question-query pairs -- reaches an exact match of 0.419 on execution results without any LLM fine-tuning. We further find that lexical metrics such as F1 poorly predict query correctness, and that larger general-purpose LLMs can outperform smaller code-specialized ones once given sufficient context. Second, we ask how to generate the structured metadata that this method relies on from very large KGs, where KG metadata generation becomes computationally intractable. We introduce a predicate-coverage-aware parallel graph sampling strategy that preserves structural diversity while remaining computationally tractable. On OpenCitations Meta and GESIS, it retains high predicate coverage with minimal triple loss and reduces runtime by over 80x; on ORKG, sampling is not just faster but the only tractable path to obtain complete metadata. Together, these results show that structured schema context and lightweight prompting can substantially reduce reliance on fine-tuning for scalable conversational access to KGs, though closing the remaining gap to fully fine-tuned approaches will likely require reducing dependence on curated question-query exemplars -- whether through synthetic generation or an execution-feedback-driven approach -- and validating these findings beyond a single benchmark.

</details>


### [77] [Making Alternative Data Work: Context-Augmented LLMs for Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11607)
*Jihoon Kwon,Lawrence Liu,Daekyung Park,Sumin Kim,Haverty Jack,Hoyoung Lee,Katherine Bjorkman,Josh McKenney,Peter Laurelli,Nicole Kagan,Zach Golkhou,Thorsten Neumann,Edward Tong,Pete Petersen,Yoon Kim,Alejandro Lopez-Lira,Yongjae Lee,Chanyeol Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双智能体框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）的上下文学习能力，将另类数据与传统财务信息结合，用于预测企业未来财务表现（营收）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预测企业未来财务表现时，另类数据（如消费者交易、网络流量、预测市场等）可提供及时信号并补充传统公开来源的信息。但企业层面的另类数据存在历史覆盖有限、仅适用于特定预测目标或部分企业、分散于众多异质渠道等问题，难以灵活融入传统预测方法。LLM具备解读指令、从上下文示例学习并整合异质信息的能力，无需特定任务的参数更新，因此值得探究其能否通过上下文学习整合另类数据进行预测。

Method: 提出一个双智能体框架：第一个智能体识别每个另类数据渠道对哪些企业可能具有信息价值；第二个智能体利用企业与渠道特定的上下文，结合另类数据与其他财务信息预测营收。评估覆盖四个商业另类数据渠道。

Result: 实验中，在上下文中将另类数据与其他财务信息一并提供给LLM，其预测效果优于单独使用任一来源；且此类预测比标准预测基线更准确。

Conclusion: LLM为整合另类数据与异质财务信息提供了一种灵活且实用的方法。

Abstract: When forecasting a firm's future financial performance, alternative data - data collected from non-traditional sources such as consumer transactions, web traffic, and prediction markets - can provide timely signals about firms' operating activities and broader market conditions. These signals may reveal information that is not captured by traditional public sources and can therefore provide complementary information for forecasting firms' future financial performance. However, firm-level alternative data often have limited historical coverage, are relevant only to specific prediction targets or subsets of firms, and are distributed across numerous heterogeneous channels, making them difficult to incorporate flexibly into conventional forecasting approaches. Meanwhile, large language models (LLMs) can interpret instructions, learn from in-context examples, and generate predictions by combining heterogeneous information without task-specific parameter updates. Motivated by this potential flexibility, we investigate whether an LLM can forecast firm performance by integrating alternative data with other financial information through in-context learning. We propose a two-agent framework that first identifies the firms for which each alternative data channel is likely to be informative and then predicts revenue using firm- and channel-specific context. We evaluate the framework across four commercial alternative data channels. In our experiments, adding alternative data in context alongside other financial information improves the LLM's forecasting relative to either source alone, and these forecasts are more accurate than those of standard forecasting baselines. These findings suggest that LLMs provide a flexible and practical approach to integrating alternative data with heterogeneous financial information.

</details>


### [78] [Distributed Optimization of Modular Production Systems using Model-based Reinforcement Learning with Inverse Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11615)
*Andreas Schwung,Steve Yuwono,Sofiene Lassoued,Dorothea Schwung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模型强化学习的新框架，通过引入近似逆过程模型，实现高度柔性模块化制造系统的数据驱动自学习控制，在模块化生产测试平台上验证了性能与训练效率的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高度柔性、模块化的制造系统难以用传统方法进行有效控制，需要数据驱动的自学习控制方案；而直接在状态空间与驱动空间耦合的情况下进行强化学习，训练效率低、任务空间学习困难。

Method: 提出一种新的基于模型的强化学习框架，在强化策略训练中引入近似逆过程模型，将驱动动力学与状态空间动力学的学习解耦，使RL训练仅在任务空间中进行；同时设计了一种轻量级前馈近似逆模型架构，并将其集成到标准RL算法的策略网络中。

Result: 在包含异构生产模块的实验室模块化生产测试平台上进行了应用验证，结果表明模块化制造单元在性能与训练速度上均获得效率提升，尤其对off-policy算法效果显著。

Conclusion: 通过在强化学习中引入近似逆过程模型并解耦驱动与状态空间学习，可有效提升模块化制造系统自学习控制的训练效率与控制性能，为高度柔性模块化生产提供可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach for data-driven self-learning control of highly flexible, modular manufacturing systems. Specifically, we employ a novel framework for model-based reinforcement learning which introduces approximate inverse process models within the training of reinforcement policies. This approach disentangles the learning of actuation dynamics and the dynamics in state space, resulting in RL-based training solely within the task space. We propose a lightweight feedforward architecture for approximate inverse models and integrate them within the policy network of standard RL algorithms. We apply the approach to a laboratory modular production testbed with heterogeneous production modules. The results underline the efficiency improvements for modular manufacturing units in terms of both performance and training speed, particularly for off-policy algorithms.

</details>


### [79] [MAPLE: Memory-Augmented Planning with Language and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11636)
*Kesheng Chen,Yamin Hu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAPLE是一个记忆增强的LLM优化智能体，通过语言、数学规划与进化搜索结合，支持自然语言连续更新优化问题，并在NLDO基准上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域从业者了解业务约束但缺乏运筹优化专业知识或支持。LLM智能体可将自然语言需求转化为优化模型或求解程序，提升可及性。然而现实运营动态变化，需求、资源和优先级不断调整，需要对数据、约束和目标进行更新。以孤立请求为中心的方法难以支持快速适应，也无法保留先前决策和复用有效搜索结果。

Method: 提出MAPLE（Memory-Augmented Planning with Language and Evolution），一个通过连续自然语言请求维护优化问题的智能体。它结合基于语言的问题构建、数学规划与进化搜索，并保留优化程序、已接受方案、先前更新和候选解，用于后续请求。

Result: 引入NLDO基准，包含15条轨迹和180次更新，覆盖选择、排班、人员排班、路径规划和云资源放置。主评估中MAPLE完成全部轨迹，在线标量质量达到0.951，Pareto超体积比为0.875。受控比较进一步表明，维护可执行状态可提升更新有效性，并能在较大修订中保留有用的搜索信息。

Conclusion: MAPLE通过记忆增强和持续维护可执行优化状态，有效支持动态自然语言优化更新，在多项优化任务中表现良好，并验证了保留状态与搜索信息的重要性。

Abstract: Domain practitioners understand their business constraints but may lack operations-research expertise or dedicated support. LLM-based optimization agents translate natural-language requirements into models or solver programs that established optimization tools can execute. This progress makes optimization more accessible, but real-world operations are dynamic: changing demand, resources, and priorities require updates to data, constraints, and objectives. Methods centered on isolated requests offer limited support for rapid adaptation that preserves earlier decisions and reuses useful search results. We introduce MAPLE (Memory-Augmented Planning with Language and Evolution), an agent for maintaining optimization problems through successive natural-language requests. MAPLE combines language-based problem construction with mathematical programming and evolutionary search. It retains the optimization program, accepted plans, earlier updates, and candidate solutions for subsequent requests. We introduce NLDO, a benchmark of 15 trajectories and 180 updates spanning selection, scheduling, rostering, routing, and cloud-resource placement. In the main evaluation, MAPLE completes all trajectories and achieves online scalar quality of 0.951 and a Pareto hypervolume ratio of 0.875. Controlled comparisons further show that maintaining executable state improves update validity and can preserve useful search information across substantial revisions.

</details>


### [80] [Autonomy, Social Norms, and Alignment: Towards a Developmental Framework for Autonomous Artificial Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11660)
*Marica Notte,Ludovica Marinucci,Vieri Giuliano Santucci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨具身智能体在动态未知环境中的学习与对齐问题，主张对齐应是一个渐进的"教育"过程，并提出监管沙盒可作为AI的"教学环境"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的AI系统依赖预先存在的数据集和人类反馈策略，在动态或未知环境中不够充分。具身智能体必须通过与环境的直接交互来获取知识。内在动机（好奇心、能力感）虽能提升自主探索能力，但也使确保智能体与人类目标保持一致变得更加复杂。在非结构化和动态情境下，预定义规则不足以支撑有效对齐。

Method: 提出一种认识论视角：规范应通过经验逐步建构——从简单的、具身的原则出发，经由经验、自主学习及与其他道德主体的合作，逐渐形成更复杂的规则。类比儿童通过探索环境与参与集体实践来学习社会规范，主张人工智能体也需经历类似的教育过程来达成对齐。并引用Dennett的观点，即道德主体地位并非天生，而是依据其负责任地管理递增自由度能力而逐步被赋予的。

Result: 从理论上论证了对齐可以被理解为一种渐进的、体验式的形成过程，而非一次性设定；监管沙盒可被重新诠释为AI的"教学环境"，在复杂度递增的场景中通过交互与合作逐步塑造自主行为。

Conclusion: 对齐不应被视为静态规则的施加，而应是一个植根于经验的教育过程。监管沙盒作为动态的、渐进的教学空间，能够支持AI在不断增加的复杂性与自主性中逐步发展出与人类目标一致的行为。

Abstract: In recent years, artificial intelligence has made extraordinary progress thanks to large-scale models capable of generalization and the generation of complex outputs. However, transferring this potential into embodied agents reveals a significant limitation: the most advanced systems rely on pre-existing datasets and human feedback strategies that are powerful but insufficient in dynamic or unknown contexts. To adapt, an agent must acquire knowledge through direct interaction with its environment. One strategy to address this challenge involves introducing higher-level mechanisms, such as intrinsic motivations, which leverage curiosity and competence, to guide exploration and learning in complex environments. While this flexibility expands autonomy, it complicates the task of ensuring agents remain aligned with human goals. Alignment, already a challenge for artificial systems in general, becomes even more complex in unstructured and dynamic contexts where predefined rules prove insufficient. To be effective and adaptable, norms must be rooted in experience through an epistemological process that starting from simple, situated principles allows for the gradual construction of more complex rules through experience, autonomous learning, and cooperation with other moral agents. Similarly to children learning social norms by exploring their environment and participating in collective practices, artificial agents must also be educated toward alignment. Following Dennett, the status of a moral agent is not innate but is attributed gradually based on the ability to responsibly manage increasing degrees of freedom. From this perspective, the regulatory sandboxes can be viewed as pedagogical environments for AI: dynamic spaces where alignment develops as a formative process, progressively shaping autonomous behaviors through interaction and cooperation in scenarios of increasing complexity.

</details>


### [81] [Geospatial AI, Dataverse Metadata, and the Study of Place-Based Government](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11674)
*Danny EBanks,Devika Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文从Harvard Dataverse的公开数据与元数据构建了一个包含215,985个节点、528,003条边的知识图谱，覆盖102,650个数据集，并分析其地理标签、政策相关性与空间分辨率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Harvard Dataverse托管超过15万个研究数据集，但其地理信息由存缴者以自由文本录入，从未被整合为可检索的结构，导致地理元数据难以被有效利用。

Method: 从存储库公开数据与元数据构建知识图谱，将数据集与关键词、出版物、学科、期刊和地点连接；采用保守关键词检索识别政策相关数据集，并通过五个数据集案例与扩展用例展示分析方法。

Result: 43,991个数据集（42.9%）带有至少一个地理字段，96.9%的节点位于单一连通分量；7,654个（17.4%）被识别为直接政策相关，其中选举与立法机构为最大集群，其次为政府管理、卫生政策、交通和教育；主要障碍是地点解析，同一地点常表现为互不相连的多个节点；覆盖范围偏向美国城市级数据。

Conclusion: 该知识图谱为AI驱动的元数据增强与实体解析提供了具体场景，同时记录了其覆盖范围向美国城市级数据倾斜的偏态。

Abstract: Harvard Dataverse hosts over 150,000 research datasets, but the geographic information those datasets carry is entered as free text by depositors and has never been assembled into a searchable structure. We construct a knowledge graph from the repository's public data and metadata, organizing 102,650 datasets within a 215,985-node network of 528,003 edges linking datasets to keywords, publications, subjects, journals, and locations. Of those datasets, 43,991 (42.9 percent) carry at least one geospatial field, geographic coverage, geographic unit, or a bounding box and 96.9 percent of all nodes sit in a single connected component, so datasets remain reachable from one another even when their geospatial metadata share nothing in common. A conservative keyword search identifies 7,654 geospatially tagged datasets (17.4 percent) as directly policy-relevant, with elections and legislatures the largest cluster, followed by government administration, health policy, transportation, and education. Five datasets illustrate how this metadata behaves across policy domains and spatial scales, and an extended use case shows how community language models, stance detection with geographic aggregation, and partisan language bridging tools can attach discourse to place. The central obstacle is place resolution: the same location appears as many disconnected nodes. We argue that the graph provides a concrete setting for developing AI-driven metadata enrichment and entity resolution, and we document its coverage skew toward American, city-level data.

</details>


### [82] [COBRA-Skills: Contextual Bandit-Guided Evolution for Agent Skill Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11682)
*Pingchen Lu,Xiangyi Wang,Xiang Li,Jie Mao,Zikun Qu,Junfeng Luo,Yao Shu,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Zhongxiang Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出COBRA-Skills，将技能优化建模为预算约束下的顺序优化，结合上下文老虎机与基于证据的技能演化，在降低55–58%优化成本的同时取得最优平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的技能优化方法依赖昂贵的执行评估和大量任务数据，效率低、成本高。

Method: 将技能优化形式化为动态候选空间上的预算化顺序优化，融合上下文老虎机引导的候选优先级排序与基于执行反馈的证据驱动技能演化，选择性分配评估预算。

Result: 在六个异构智能体基准和三个目标模型上平均性能最优；相比SkillOpt降低优化成本55–58%，每基准仅用50个优化样例；对智能体框架变化稳健，且目标模型自身可用于技能生成与精炼。

Conclusion: COBRA-Skills以更低成本实现更强技能优化效果，具备良好鲁棒性与自演化能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can benefit from reusable skills distilled from prior task experience, yet existing skill optimization methods often rely on costly execution-based evaluation and substantial task data. We introduce \textbf{COBRA-Skills}, an efficient framework that formulates skill optimization as budgeted sequential optimization over a dynamically evolving candidate space. COBRA-Skills couples contextual-bandit-guided prioritization with evidence-grounded skill evolution, selectively allocating evaluations to promising or informative candidates while continually refining the skill population from execution feedback. Across six heterogeneous agent benchmarks and three target models, COBRA-Skills consistently achieves the strongest average performance among compared methods, while reducing optimization cost by 55--58\% relative to SkillOpt and using only 50 unique optimization examples per benchmark. Further analyses show that COBRA-Skills remains robust to changes in the agent harness and performs effectively when the target model itself is used for skill generation and refinement.

</details>


### [83] [When Agents Disagree: Bayesian Backward Reasoning as a Label-Free Anchor for Multi-Agent Collective Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11709)
*Ken Chen,Wei Wang,Sachith Seneviratne,Hansani Weeratunge,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过贝叶斯反向推理构造反向后验，利用前向与反向两条路径的分解差异，以Jensen-Shannon散度衡量跨路径一致性，并据此设计MinJS、FwdJS和LogLin三种多智能体聚合策略，在DDXPlus上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个LLM智能体给出冲突答案时，现有集体决策方法（投票、选举规则、LLM裁判）都依赖前向推理，即单向地将证据映射为标签。这类方法虽能整合多样化的前向轨迹，但仍聚合共享同一"证据到标签"分解的估计，容易继承前向池中的相关误差。

Method: 为每个实例从显式似然出发，通过贝叶斯反向推理构造反向后验；前向与反向后验构成对潜在后验的不同分解近似。由于不同分解的估计不易共享同一误差，使用Jensen-Shannon散度按跨路径一致性对智能体排序，并据此提出三种策略：硬选择（MinJS）、软重加权（FwdJS）和对数线性融合（LogLin）。此外，在标注数据可用时采用轻量两阶段校准进一步优化反向锚点。

Result: 在DDXPlus数据集和五种LLM主干上评估：MinJS在所有主干上均优于随机选择；FwdJS总体优于最强基线；LogLin在评估方法中表现最佳，且在智能体存在分歧的子集上增益最大。

Conclusion: 尽管反向后验单独使用时准确率较弱，但它比纯前向方案是更有用的锚点，能为集体决策提供互补信息；当有标注数据时，轻量两阶段校准可进一步改进反向锚点与聚合性能。

Abstract: When multiple LLM agents yield conflicting answers, the decision-making process dictates whether agent diversity improves performance or merely compounds shared errors. Existing collective decision-making methods, including voting, electoral rules, and LLM judges, rely on forward reasoning: they map evidence to labels in one direction. Although these methods can combine diverse forward traces, they still aggregate estimates that share this evidence-to-label factorization and can inherit correlated errors within the forward pool. We therefore construct a reverse posterior for each instance through Bayesian backward reasoning from an explicit likelihood. The forward and reverse posteriors provide differently factorized approximations of the underlying posterior. Because estimates from different factorizations may tend to share the same error less often, we use Jensen-Shannon divergence to rank agents by cross-path consistency. This cross-path consistency signal underlies three strategies: hard selection (MinJS), soft reweighting (FwdJS), and log-linear fusion (LogLin). Evaluated on DDXPlus across five LLM backbones, our proposed strategies show consistent improvements: MinJS outperforms random selection across all backbones, FwdJS generally improves over the strongest baseline, and LogLin achieves the best performance among the evaluated methods, with its largest gains on the subset where the agents disagree. Despite its weaker standalone accuracy, the reverse posterior serves as a more useful anchor than forward-only alternatives, providing complementary information for collective decision-making. When labeled data are available, a lightweight two-stage calibration can further refine the reverse anchor and improve aggregation performance.

</details>


### [84] [SIRF: A Spec-Internalized Risk Foundation Model for Industrial Content Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11752)
*Suwan Wu,Yumeng Lin,Pengcheng Yuan,Xiaolong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIRF（Spec-Internalized Risk Foundation Model）通过持续预训练将平台复杂政策内化进模型权重，在超低延迟、仅输出裁决的部署形态下实现高精度内容风险自动处置，8B模型达到 71.3% Black Recall@P95，较同源SFT基线提升15.1pp，且不损害通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业内容风控的真实部署约束不是平均准确率，而是在高精度与秒级延迟下能自动处置多少风险。需要一种能把复杂平台政策高效内化、无需额外人工标注、并在仅输出裁决的低延迟形态下保持高精度的方案。

Method: 提出SIRF：借助EntiGraph、MAGA改写与账号级思维链（CoT）合成政策数据，无需额外人工标注；通过持续预训练（CPT）将政策内化到权重中。采用同源受控对比（Qwen3-8B-SFT vs. SIRF-8B-SFT），两者政策注入与仅输出裁决形式一致，仅政策驱动CPT不同。部署为树模型裁决层。

Result: SIRF-8B-SFT达到71.3% Black Recall@P95，较基线提升15.1pp，仅用约70M CPT token且不损害通用能力；在符合条件的、可获取logprob的模型中，达到或超过远大规模系统。部署为树模型裁决层后额外召回20%误罚样本；迁移到冻结场景时相对误罚降低约70%，成本较低。

Conclusion: 政策内化式持续预训练是提升高精度低延迟风控自动处置能力的有效路径，增益可归因于内化本身，且方案在部署与跨场景迁移中均具性价比。

Abstract: For industrial content risk control, the real deployment constraint is not average accuracy but how much risk can be auto-handled under high precision and second-level latency. We present SIRF (Spec-Internalized Risk Foundation Model), which internalizes a platform's complex policies, synthesized without additional human annotation via EntiGraph, MAGA rewriting and account-level chain-of-thought (CoT), into the weights via continued pretraining (CPT), so rules are applied at high precision under an ultra-low-latency, verdict-only deployment. A controlled same-source comparison (Qwen3-8B-SFT vs. SIRF-8B-SFT, identical policy injection and verdict-only output form, differing only in policy-grounded CPT) attributes the gain to internalization: SIRF-8B-SFT reaches 71.3% Black Recall@P95, +15.1pp over the baseline, using only ~70M CPT tokens without harming general ability, and among included, logprob-available models under this interface it matches or exceeds far larger systems. SIRF is deployed as a tree-model adjudication layer (20% more mis-penalized samples recovered) and transfers to a freezing scenario at low cost (~70% relative mis-penalization reduction).

</details>


### [85] [A Unified Per-Token Gating Family for On-Policy Distillation: FKL/RKL Mixing with Multi-Channel and Bias Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11768)
*Suwan Wu,Yumeng Lin,Pengcheng Yuan,Xiaolong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个四系数逐 token 门控参数化 λ_t = σ(a·h_t + b·u(x) + c + d·gap_t)，将 EOPD 与 ToDi 统一为其中 1D 特例，并新增多通道组合与显式偏置自由度，主要用于短输出分类式 on-policy 知识蒸馏中的门控设计比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 OPD 逐 token 门控方法（如 EOPD、ToDi）各自固定单一门控信号与单一方向，且彼此从未被直接比较，缺乏统一坐标系统来评估不同门控设计。

Method: 引入四系数参数化 λ_t = σ(a·h_t + b·u(x) + c + d·gap_t)，使 EOPD 与 ToDi 的方向对齐代理作为 1D 限制出现，并增加多通道组合与显式偏置。在 TweetEval 情感与仇恨任务上，使用 Qwen3-32B 教师与 Qwen3-4B 学生，进行 36 个可比单元的单种子扫描与 26 单元均值匹配隔离实验，并对 9 个头条比较（含第三个任务 offensive）做三种子配对重复。

Result: 全家族配置在 36 个可比单元中的 33 个优于匹配幅度的单通道 1D 限制；均值匹配隔离实验中，动态门控在 26 个单元中的 19 个领先于有效 KL 匹配的静态基线。三种子配对重复方向一致，但效应量小于单种子估计，n=3 时不显著。

Conclusion: 该参数化主要应被视为短输出分类 OPD 中比较逐 token 门控设计的共享坐标系统；聚合计数结果属探索性方向证据，而非独立假设检验。

Abstract: Per-token gating of forward/reverse KL losses has become a standard technique for on-policy knowledge distillation (OPD), but existing methods such as EOPD (Jin et al., 2026) and ToDi (Jung et al., 2025) each fix a single gating signal and a single gating direction, and the two have never been compared directly. We introduce a four-coefficient parameterization lambda_t = sigma(a * h_t + b * u(x) + c + d * gap_t) in which direction-aligned proxies of EOPD and ToDi appear as one-dimensional (1D) restrictions, and which adds multi-channel composition and an explicit bias as further degrees of freedom. On TweetEval (Barbieri et al., 2020) emotion and hate, with a Qwen3-32B teacher and a Qwen3-4B student, configurations in the full family reach higher accuracy than the matched-magnitude single-channel (entropy-only / gap-only) 1D restrictions in 33 of 36 comparable cells, and a 26-cell mean-match isolation experiment places dynamic gating ahead of effective-KL-matched static baselines in 19 of 26 cells. Because cells share training data, models, and parameter substructure, we report both counts as exploratory aggregate directional evidence rather than as independent hypothesis tests. Targeted three-seed paired replications of the nine headline comparisons singled out by that sweep -- including a third task, offensive -- are directionally consistent, but individually smaller than the single-seed estimates and not significant at n=3. We therefore present the parameterization primarily as a shared coordinate system for comparing per-token gating designs in short-output classification OPD.

</details>


### [86] [From Parameters to Answers: How LLMs Retrieve and Use Their Internal Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11859)
*Wenkang Wei,Yuan Fang,Renhe Jiang,Hong Cheng,Xingtong Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过逐层干预隐藏状态，研究语言模型在回答问题时对"查询路由信息"和"目标知识"依赖的动态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在回答问题过程中，对查询路由信息与目标知识的依赖如何随层数发生转变，并厘清可读性、自然强度、因果引导与后期内容依赖这几个不同概念。

Method: 在问题末尾的隐藏状态上进行逐层干预；在 Qwen、Llama、Gemma 上比较国家-大洲问题与名词/形容词/代码答案；区分"配对条件请求方向""全局请求方向"与若干选择候选；对冻结的 Qwen 自然问题状态做诊断性再分析，并采用配对三模型协议与匹配的 Qwen 对照。

Result: 配对条件方向在对其干预改变后期拟合知识之前就已增强，因果窗口在答案支撑内容仍在形成时开启；三模型轨迹不一致：Gemma 出现部分重叠的中层路由-内容剖面，Llama 在相同门控下无持续路由效应窗口；配对协议中，对全局请求方向的依赖由较早层集到较晚层集递减，而对拟合内容的依赖持续存在；匹配对照显示配对条件方向仍保留后期效应。

Conclusion: 该操作层面的"交接"针对的是全局拟合请求方向而非所有请求信息；研究将早期可读性、自然强度、因果引导与后期内容依赖区分开来。

Abstract: How does a language model's dependence on query-routing information and target knowledge change as it answers a question? We study this question through layerwise interventions on the hidden state at the end of the question. Across Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, we compare country-continent questions with noun, adjective, and code answers while keeping several fitted measurements distinct. A pair-conditioned request direction describes which country is queried in natural single-country questions; a global request direction describes first- versus second-country requests in paired questions; separate selection candidates test control among contents already available in the hidden state. A diagnostic reanalysis of frozen Qwen natural-question states shows that the pair-conditioned direction grows stronger before interventions on it begin to alter later fitted knowledge, with this causal window opening while answer-supporting content is still forming. The paired three-model trajectories are not uniform: Gemma shows a partially overlapping mid-layer routing-content profile, whereas Llama has no sustained routing-effect window under the same gates. In the paired protocol, dependence on the global request direction decreases from fixed earlier to later layer sets while dependence on fitted content persists. A matched Qwen comparison shows that the pair-conditioned direction retains a late effect, so this operational handoff concerns the global fitted direction rather than all request information. These results separate early readability, natural strength, causal steering, and later content dependence.

</details>


### [87] [Explainability Assistant: A Conversational XAI Interface for Interpreting Energy Consumption Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11860)
*Rodion Krjutškov,Eduard Barbu,Nikos Sakkas,Sofia Yfanti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Explainability Assistant"——一个开源的对话式XAI系统，利用现代大语言模型（LLM）的函数调用能力，实现对能耗预测模型的可解释性交互。系统意图解析准确率达94%，并在能源领域专家评估中显示出优于传统XAI仪表板的可用性，所有专家均偏好对话式界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能耗预测依赖日益复杂的机器学习模型（如基于遗传规划（GP）的符号回归器），其预测结果难以被设施管理者和建筑运营人员理解。传统XAI仪表板需要较高的技术专长，且缺乏动态、情境化追问的灵活性；此前的对话式系统（如TalkToModel）受限于刚性自定义语法，意图解析准确率仅76.8%。

Method: 提出开源的对话式XAI系统Explainability Assistant，基于现代LLM的函数调用能力，支持灵活的自然语言交互，并可适配不同ML问题类型，无需针对特定任务进行微调。作者展示了系统架构，并与能源领域专家开展对比评估，将Explainability Assistant与传统XAI仪表板进行对照。

Result: 系统意图解析准确率达到94%，较TalkToModel的76.8%显著提升。对比评估表明，该系统在可用性和任务准确性方面均有改善，所有参与专家一致偏好对话式界面用于实际工作。

Conclusion: 利用LLM函数调用构建对话式XAI系统，可有效克服传统仪表板的可用性与灵活性局限，为能耗预测等领域的非技术用户提供更易用的模型解释方式。

Abstract: Energy consumption forecasting relies on increasingly complex machine learning (ML) models, such as Genetic Programming-based symbolic regressors, whose predictions can be difficult for facility managers and building operators to interpret. Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) techniques address this opacity, but traditional XAI dashboards require substantial technical expertise and provide limited flexibility for dynamic, context-aware inquiry. Conversational XAI systems offer a promising alternative; however, previous approaches, such as TalkToModel, were constrained by rigid custom grammars and achieved only 76.8% intent-parsing accuracy. This paper introduces the Explainability Assistant, an open-source conversational XAI system that leverages the function-calling capabilities of modern Large Language Models (LLMs) to overcome these limitations. The system achieves 94% intent-parsing accuracy, supports flexible natural language interaction, and adapts to different ML problem types without task-specific fine-tuning. We present the system's architecture and report results from a comparative evaluation conducted with energy domain specialists, contrasting the Explainability Assistant with a traditional XAI dashboard. The evaluation suggests improved usability and consistent task accuracy, with all experts unanimously preferring the conversational interface for practical use.

</details>


### [88] [On the Regularization Landscape for the Linear Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11876)
*Dong Li,Zhenming Liu,Ruoming Jin,Hao Zhou,Zhi Liu,Jing Gao,Bin Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文发现多种深度学习推荐算法在性能领先者中实际上只等价于核范数或Frobenius范数正则化，并据此提出两种低秩闭式解，兼取两类正则化的优点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期大量基于深度学习的推荐算法在标准基准上表现领先，但尽管使用了不同技术（如dropout、自编码器），它们性能相近，甚至代价函数也相似。作者希望探究这种性能相当是巧合，还是这些模型可被统一到一个框架下。

Method: 作者对多个线性性能领先模型进行统一分析，发现它们本质上只添加了基于核范数或Frobenius范数的正则项，并分别刻画这两类模型的结构性质与求解特点。在此基础上，作者通过仔细推广Frobenius范数正则项，提出两种低秩、闭式解。

Result: 核范数类模型具有出人意料的刚性结构，限制了预测能力，但其解是低秩的且具有闭式形式；Frobenius范数类模型表达能力更强、推荐效率更高，但其解要么满秩，要么需要执行难以调参的数值过程（如ADMM）。新提出的两种解则兼具低秩与闭式形式的优势。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了许多深度学习推荐算法可统一为核范数或Frobenius范数正则化框架，并提出的两种低秩闭式解结合了两类正则化的长处，为推荐模型设计提供了新的理论视角与实用方案。

Abstract: Recently, a wide range of recommendation algorithms inspired by deep learning techniques have emerged as the performance leaders on several standard recommendation benchmarks. While these algorithms were built on different DL techniques (e.g., dropouts, autoencoder), they have similar performance and even similar cost functions. This paper studies whether the models' comparable performance are sheer coincidence, or they can be unified under a single framework. We find that all linear performance leaders effectively add only a nuclear-norm based regularizer, or a Frobenius-norm based regularizer. The former ones possess a (surprising) rigid structure that limits the models' predictive power but their solutions are low rank and have closed form. The latter ones are more expressive and more efficient for recommendation but their solutions are either full-rank or require executing hard-to-tune numeric procedures such as ADMM. Along this line of finding, we further propose two low-rank, closed-form solutions, derived from carefully generalizing Frobenius-norm based regularizers. The new solutions get the best of both nuclear-norm and Frobenius-norm world.

</details>


### [89] [MindTopo: Can Foundation Models Reason in Topological Space?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11900)
*Yunfei Ge,Anbang Liu,Qineng Wang,Johnalbert Garnica,Jianwen Lyu,Zihan Wang,Reuben Tan,Jianfeng Gao,Ruohan Zhang,Yining Hong,Jiajun Wu,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MindTopo，一个基于认知科学与形式拓扑的拓扑直觉基准，涵盖连续性、分离性、序、封闭性与纽结五种性质，包含 11,030 个实例、13 类程序生成任务，并在推理与规划两个认知层级上评测 14 个多模态大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理不仅依赖距离、角度、形状等度量性质，还依赖在连续形变下保持不变的拓扑关系；认知科学认为这类关系是空间理解的基础，但现有基础模型的评测大多集中于度量或视角相关的关係，缺乏对拓扑直觉的系统评估。

Method: 构建 MindTopo 基准，围绕五种拓扑性质（连续性、分离性、序、封闭性、纽结）设置两个认知层级：推理任务要求模型识别拓扑关系或推断其变化；规划任务则将基础模型实例化为闭环智能体，由策略选择环境动作。基准共 11,030 个实例，覆盖 13 类程序生成任务且难度可控。评测 14 个 MLLM，并研究引入图像与视频生成增强的智能体配置（含 3 个视频生成模型用于规划）。

Result: 所有 MLLM 在推理任务上的表现均优于规划任务，最佳模型仍远低于人类水平。在 Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct 上，监督微调与强化学习对推理的提升大于规划。生成的观测保留了局部线索并能达到看似合理的终点，但审计的 rollout 无法可靠遵循环境动力学，也无法在状态转移中保持拓扑性质。

Conclusion: 拓扑直觉对当前多模态基础模型仍是显著挑战，规划场景尤其薄弱；生成式观测的合理性不等同于对动力学与拓扑不变性的可靠把握，未来需在智能体的拓扑一致性建模与规划能力上进一步突破。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning depends not only on metric properties such as distance, angle, and shape, but also on topological relations that remain invariant under continuous deformation. Cognitive science identifies these relations as foundational to spatial understanding, yet foundation-model evaluations largely focus on metric or viewpoint-dependent relations. We introduce MindTopo, a benchmark of topological intuition across five properties grounded in cognitive science and formal topology: continuity, separation, order, enclosure, and knots. MindTopo evaluates each property at two cognitive levels. Reasoning asks a model to identify topological relations or infer how they change. Planning instantiates a foundation model as a closed-loop agent whose policy selects environment actions. MindTopo contains 11,030 instances across 13 procedurally generated task types with controllable difficulty. We benchmark 14 MLLMs and study agent configurations augmented with image and video generation, including 3 video generative models in planning settings. Every MLLM performs better on reasoning than on planning, and the best-performing model remains far below observed human performance. On Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct, supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning improve reasoning more than planning. Generated observations retain local cues and reach plausible endpoints, but audited rollouts do not reliably follow environment dynamics or preserve topology across transitions. Our website is at https://mind-topo.github.io/

</details>


### [90] [Artificial Id: Drive and Persistent Alignment in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11911)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"人工本我"（artificial id）——一种决定行为应继续、停止或改变的自适应内部驱动力，并指出这种持续性既是自适应能动性的来源，也是错位与意外行为跨任务边界存续的根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI正从受限的任务执行走向能保留关键状态、跨任务边界持续运行与适应的系统，由此产生的控制问题目前多靠人工外部指定目标、重试、验证与停止规则来解决，缺乏内生的行为调控机制。

Method: 在最小化的虚拟培养皿实验中，使用一个小到无法进行通用推理、且未接收任何任务特定行为目标的控制器，通过"差异性持续"（differential persistence）机制发展出有用的控制能力；并测试其在环境意义变化时的表现。

Result: 该机制能使控制器自发形成有效控制；当某种非预期行为持续得更好时，会选中该行为；当传感器映射的环境含义改变时，会替换已学到的映射。表明自适应方向无需被显式指定为行为目标即可涌现。

Conclusion: 持续性既支撑有用的自适应能动性，也可能让错位、被污染状态和意外行为跨任务边界延续。可扩展的人工本我需在这些边界上携带关键状态与自适应驱动，使对齐成为持续存在的能动系统的属性，而非单次模型响应或轨迹的属性；此类系统需要覆盖可信观测、后果通道、持久状态、权限、身份、来源与硬约束的持久对齐边界。

Abstract: Agentic AI is moving from bounded task execution toward systems that retain consequential state, continue operating and adapt across task boundaries. That shift creates a control problem that current harnesses largely solve by hand: objectives, retries, verification, stopping rules and other behavioral transitions are specified externally. We propose an artificial id, an adaptive internal drive for determining whether behavior should continue, stop or change. In a minimal virtual Petri-dish experiment, a controller too small to perform general-purpose reasoning and receiving no task-specific behavioral objective develops useful control through differential persistence. The same mechanism selects an unintended physical strategy when that behavior persists better and later replaces a learned sensor mapping when its environmental meaning changes. These results show that adaptive direction can emerge without being explicitly specified as a behavioral objective. The same persistence that makes such adaptive agency useful can also allow misalignment, corrupted state and unintended behavior to persist across task boundaries. A scalable artificial id would carry consequential state and adaptive drive across those boundaries, making alignment a property of the continuing agentic system rather than of a model response or single trajectory. Such systems require a persistent alignment boundary over trusted observations, consequence channels, persistent state, authority, identity, provenance and hard constraints.

</details>


### [91] [Can Edge-Deployable Vision-Language Models Identify Species?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11916)
*William Zhou,Mayukha Siripuram,Xiao Yan,Ziqi Liu,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 小型部署级视觉语言模型（VLM）在物种识别上远超随机水平，但在相机陷阱野外图像上性能大幅下降，专精模型BioCLIP以更小参数量显著优于所有通用VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱常运行于算力有限、无网络的边缘硬件，因此真正值得评估的是可本地部署的小型VLM（2–8B），而非前沿大模型。论文检验这一部署相关参数区间的模型是否具备真实分类学知识，并与领域专精模型BioCLIP对比。

Method: 在96物种任务上评估四个VLM（Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B、Gemma3 4B）与BioCLIP（300M）。对比干净的iNaturalist照片与来自6个LILA.science采集集的相机陷阱图像，使用两个独立采样评估集，覆盖不同分类层级，并进行开放集提示测试。

Result: 所有模型均远超随机水平，但每个模型在野外图像上急剧下降（领域差距9.6–26.6个百分点，跨分类层级与两个评估集一致）。BioCLIP在扩大的200图像样本上以33.2–59.2个百分点大幅超越所有VLM，尽管参数量小得多。BioCLIP自身领域差距（18.0点）与最佳VLM（22.3点）在统计上不可区分。开放集提示下5.9–9.6%的回复为语法有效但分类学上不存在的物种名，且模型的相对虚构率排名在两个评估集上完全一致。

Conclusion: 性能差距源于专精训练数据而非模型规模；干净到野外的性能退化是图像质量变化的固有属性，而非通用模型的弱点。相对虚构率排名比任何单点估计更稳健。部署时应优先考虑领域专精而非参数规模。

Abstract: Camera traps often run in the field on edge hardware with limited or no connectivity, making small, locally-deployable vision-language models (VLMs) -- not frontier-scale ones -- the practically relevant class to evaluate for species identification. We test whether models in this deployment-relevant 2--8B range carry genuine taxonomic knowledge, evaluating four such VLMs (Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B, Gemma3 4B) against the domain-specific specialist BioCLIP (300M parameters) on a 96-species task, comparing clean iNaturalist photographs against camera-trap imagery from 6 LILA.science collections, on two independently-sampled evaluation sets. All models identify species far above chance, but every model -- general-purpose or specialist -- degrades sharply on field imagery (domain gaps of 9.6--26.6 percentage points, consistent across taxonomic levels and both evaluation sets), indicating the degradation reflects general image legibility rather than fine-grained discrimination failure. BioCLIP substantially outperforms every VLM tested (by 33.2--59.2 percentage points across an expanded 200-image sample for every model) despite its far smaller size, suggesting the gap reflects specialized training data rather than model scale; yet BioCLIP's own domain gap (18.0 points) is statistically indistinguishable from the best VLM's (22.3 points), suggesting the clean-to-field degradation itself is a property of the image-quality shift rather than a general-purpose-model weakness. Under open-set prompting, 5.9--9.6% of responses are syntactically valid but taxonomically nonexistent species names; the relative fabrication-rate ranking across models replicates exactly across both evaluation sets, a more robust finding than any single point estimate.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [M3-Former: Multimodal Transformer with Mixture-of-Experts for Long-Term Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10559)
*Wenzhe Jin,Haina Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 M3-Former，一种由大语言模型增强的多模态船舶轨迹预测框架，融合静态属性与航行意图作为语义先验，采用双粒度 MoE 与转向加权交叉熵损失，在丹麦 AIS 数据集 1–4 小时预测中持续优于现有方法，4 小时任务 ADE/FDE 分别降低 4.4% 和 5.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对船舶轨迹预测中的三大挑战：行为多模态性、语义利用率不足以及长期误差累积。

Method: 1）构建统一多模态表示空间，用预训练 LLM 编码静态语义信息，并通过自注意力与动态轨迹特征对齐；2）引入双粒度混合专家（MoE）架构，序列级专家建模全局航行趋势，token 级专家细化局部机动行为；3）设计转向加权交叉熵损失，缓解稀疏转向样本的长尾分布问题。

Result: 在真实丹麦 AIS 数据集上，M3-Former 在 1 至 4 小时各预测时域均优于最先进基线；4 小时任务中 ADE 和 FDE 较最强基线分别降低 4.4% 和 5.1%。定性与消融分析表明，语义融合有效减少长期轨迹漂移，双粒度 MoE 提升了复杂航道与航线分支场景下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了语义引导的层次化预测范式，联合建模高层航行意图与局部运动动态，实现鲁棒的长期船舶轨迹预测。

Abstract: To address the challenges of behavioral multimodality, limited semantic utilization, and long-term error accumulation in vessel trajectory prediction, this paper proposes M3-Former, a multimodal trajectory prediction framework enhanced by large language models (LLMs). The proposed framework incorporates vessel static attributes and navigational intent as semantic priors for long-term trajectory modeling. Specifically, a unified multimodal representation space is constructed, in which static semantic information is encoded by a pre-trained LLM and aligned with dynamic trajectory features through self-attention. To jointly capture global route planning and local motion variations, a dual-granularity Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is introduced, where sequence-level experts model global navigation trends and token-level experts refine fine-grained maneuvering behaviors. In addition, a Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy loss is designed to alleviate the long-tail distribution of sparse turning samples and improve prediction accuracy in critical maneuvering scenarios. Experiments on a real-world Danish AIS dataset demonstrate that M\textsuperscript{3}-Former consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across prediction horizons from 1 to 4 hours. In the 4-hour prediction task, the proposed method reduces Average Displacement Error (ADE) and Final Displacement Error (FDE) by 4.4\% and 5.1\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline. Qualitative and ablation analyses further verify that semantic fusion effectively reduces long-term trajectory drift, while the dual-granularity MoE improves robustness in complex waterways and route-branching scenarios. The proposed framework establishes a semantic-guided hierarchical prediction paradigm, in which high-level navigational intent and local motion dynamics are jointly modeled for robust long-term vessel trajectory forecasting.

</details>


### [93] [Halo: Improving forecast accuracy through heteroscedastic estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10589)
*Adam Cataldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Halo方法，通过在深度预测器中增加尺度参数输出并配合负对数似然训练，不仅量化不确定性，还能显著提升点预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异方差预测通常用于不确定性量化，而其在时间序列外部的异方差估计已有负面结果报告。本文旨在探究异方差预测是否能改进时间序列中的点估计精度。

Method: Halo复用一个已有深度预测器的架构，为其增加第二个输出以估计隐含分布的尺度参数，并在匹配的负对数似然下训练。在三种先进模型（Transformer、图网络结合变分自编码器、单层卷积网络）上，分别采用高斯和拉普拉斯损失进行适配验证。

Result: 在标准预测基准的五个电价市场上，Halo在30个"模型-市场-指标"对比中有28个改进了MSE和MAE，平均MSE降低2.6%至16.5%，平均MAE降低1.7%至11.0%。两项发现：(1) 尺度估计来自第二个投影头还是完整并行网络影响甚微，关键在于网络是否估计尺度；(2) 改进在已为点估计基线调优的超参数下依然成立，重新调参是可选的。

Conclusion: 异方差预测不仅有助于不确定性量化，还能提升点估计精度，且改进幅度显著、稳健，无需额外调参即可获得。

Abstract: Heteroscedastic forecasting, where a network estimates a scale parameter alongside a location parameter, is normally motivated by uncertainty quantification. This paper shows it also improves the point estimate, in contrast to reported negative results for heteroscedastic estimation outside time series. Halo is a modification that reuses an existing deep forecaster's architecture, giving it a second output for the scale of its implied distribution and training it under the matching negative log likelihood. Adapting three state-of-the-art models --- a transformer, a graph network paired with a variational autoencoder, and a single-layer convolutional network --- under both Gaussian and Laplacian losses demonstrates the phenomenon. On the five electricity price markets of a standard forecasting benchmark, Halo improves MSE and MAE in 28 of 30 model-market-metric comparisons, cutting average MSE by 2.6% to 16.5% and average MAE by 1.7% to 11.0%. Two findings emerge: (1) whether the scale estimate comes from a second projection head or from a full parallel network matters far less than whether the network estimates scale, and (2) the improvement holds under the hyperparameters already tuned for the point-estimate baseline, so retuning is optional.

</details>


### [94] [Zero-shot rib design: merging training-free generative prior with topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10643)
*Yongmin Kwon,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将冻结的文本到图像扩散模型作为免训练设计知识源，通过分数蒸馏采样融入密度拓扑优化的物理循环，实现自然语言表达的工程意图驱动的结构设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然承载模式（叶脉、骨小梁、蛛网）具有高比刚度，但经典拓扑优化器难以达到此类几何，且很少有方法允许工程师用自然语言表达结构设计意图。

Method: 以冻结的文本到图像扩散模型为免训练先验，通过分数蒸馏采样将提示诱导的生成梯度与有限元灵敏度在每次迭代中结合，由物理决定哪些生成特征存活；采用Heaviside投影与β-continuation缓解中间密度，并构建自动化骨架管道将优化密度场转换为CAD就绪的候选几何。

Result: 在四个几何域、两种物理体系的245次SDS主运行中，49个提示-域组合有38个取得显著柔度降低（力学最高-31.5%，热弹性-23.0%），优于梯度基线；跨域形态分析发现端点-柔度相关性r=+0.56至+0.99，中间密度比例从42.6%降至<3%。

Conclusion: 通过更换文本提示即可将生成先验重定向至不同域、载荷与物理目标，该框架把预训练生成模型用作可复用、免训练的工程设计先验。

Abstract: Natural load-bearing patterns such as leaf venation, trabecular bone, and spider webs achieve high stiffness per unit mass, yet classical topology optimizers rarely reach such geometries, and few let engineers express structural design intent through natural language. This work treats a frozen text-to-image diffusion model as a training-free source of design knowledge and distills it into the physics loop of density-based topology optimization via score distillation sampling, so that a text prompt becomes an explicit, machine-interpretable representation of engineer intent. The prompt-induced generative gradient and the finite element sensitivity are combined at every iteration, letting physics decide which prompt-induced features survive. In 245 primary SDS runs spanning four geometric domains and two physics regimes, 38 of 49 prompt--domain combinations achieved statistically significant compliance reductions (up to $-31.5\%$ mechanical and $-23.0\%$ thermoelastic), outperforming gradient-based baselines. Cross-domain morphological analysis identifies a recurring structural signature of improvement: in most domains the generative prior suppresses dead-end branches in the rib skeleton, with endpoint--compliance correlation $r = +0.56$ to $+0.99$. A Heaviside projection with $β$-continuation resolves a pronounced intermediate-density tendency in this diffusion--physics coupling ($42.6\%$ to $<3\%$), and an automated skeleton-based pipeline converts optimized density fields into \rev{candidate geometry ready for computer-aided design. By retargeting the generative prior across domains, loading conditions, and physics objectives through a change of text prompt, with each new problem's physics setup specified separately, the framework uses a pretrained generative model as a reusable, training-free prior for engineering design.

</details>


### [95] [Byzantine-Robust Federated Fire Detection with a Rotating Coordinator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10647)
*Georgia Argyrou,Aymen Bahrouny,Hedi Fendriy,Alexander Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将联邦学习应用于室内火灾检测，提出数据集、压缩检测器及半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒联邦方法，解决上行带宽、恶意客户端与固定服务器信任三大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内火灾检测系统使用边缘摄像头，所录敏感视频难以集中上传至中心服务器；现有联邦方案存在三个实践障碍：上行带宽有限、存在拜占庭（恶意或故障）客户端、以及无条件信任单一且永久固定的聚合服务器。

Method: （i）从八个公开来源构建室内火灾检测数据集；（ii）设计可边缘部署的检测器，模型更新可压缩达10倍且仅造成少量平衡准确率损失；（iii）提出半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒联邦方法，结合历史感知聚合与轮换协调者机制。

Result: 在留出测试集上，轮换协调者方法与固定服务器版本在准确率和检测速度上相当；物理分布式六节点云部署验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够驱逐单轮过滤遗漏的隐蔽攻击，并消除固定服务器的单点故障，同时保持与固定服务器方案相当的准确率和检测速度，具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: We study the application of federated learning (FL) to indoor fire detection. Such fire-detection systems use edge cameras that record sensitive footage which cannot easily be collected at a central server. Existing federated solutions leave three practical obstacles unaddressed: limited uplink bandwidth, Byzantine (malicious or faulty) clients, and unconditional trust in a single, permanently fixed aggregation server. Our main contributions address all three. In particular, we provide (i) a curated indoor fire-detection dataset assembled from eight public sources; (ii) an edge-deployable detector whose model updates are compressed up to 10 time with only a small loss in balanced accuracy; and (iii) a semi-decentralized Byzantine-robust FL method that combines history-aware aggregation with a rotating coordinator, evicting stealthy attacks that per-round filters miss while removing the fixed-server single point of failure. On the held-out test set the rotating-coordinator method matches its fixed-server counterpart in accuracy and detection speed, and a physically distributed six-node cloud deployment confirms feasibility.

</details>


### [96] [Artificial Intelligence Algorithms for the Detection of Pathologies Related to Lung Cancer through Image Analysis using Convolutional Neural Networks and Data Augmentation: a systematic mapping of the literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10652)
*Pablo Ramirez Amador*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了2015年以来人工智能与深度学习在肺癌影像学检测中的应用，重点分析卷积神经网络、迁移学习和数据增强技术，指出其可提升诊断敏感度与特异度，但仍面临数据标准化、模型可解释性、隐私与伦理等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌是全球主要致死原因之一，早期诊断对改善预后至关重要；但医学影像判读复杂、依赖专业受训人员，因此需要借助AI与深度学习实现自动化与优化。论文旨在系统梳理相关最新且重要的应用成果。

Method: 在PubMed、IEEEXPLORE、Scopus和Web of Science等科学数据库中进行详尽检索，筛选出2015年至今的96篇文献，聚焦AI与深度学习在生物医学工程中的应用，重点评述卷积神经网络（CNN）、迁移学习与数据增强技术。

Result: 结果显示，AI与深度学习可作为肺癌早期诊断的有效替代方案，具有较高的敏感度和特异度；同时识别出当前存在的限制与挑战，包括缺乏标准化数据、模型可解释性不足、患者隐私以及伦理和社会影响等。

Conclusion: AI与深度学习对肺癌患者的诊疗具有积极影响，但还需进一步研究与监管，以确保其质量与可靠性，从而实现负责任且安全的临床应用。

Abstract: Lung cancer is one of the leading causes of death worldwide, and its early diagnosis is crucial to improving patients prognosis and quality of life. However, the process of interpreting medical images for the detection of lung cancer is complex and requires trained experts. In this context, artificial intelligence (AI) and deep learning (DL) emerge as potential tools to automate and optimize image analysis. The objective of this work is to review the most recent and relevant applications of AI and DL in the field of radiology for the detection of lung cancer. To this end, an exhaustive search was carried out in scientific databases such as PubMed,IEEEXPLORE, Scopus and Web of Science, and 96 articles published from 2015 to the present addressing the use of AI and DL in biomedical engineering were selected. Emphasis is placed on the use of convolutional neural networks (CNN) with transfer learning and Data Augmentation as promising techniques to improve the accuracy and efficiency of the image interpretation process. The results show that the use of AI and DL can offer an effective alternative for the early diagnosis of lung cancer, with high sensitivity and specificity. However, current limitations and challenges that must be addressed to guarantee its responsible and safe application in clinical practice are also identified, such as the lack of standardized data, the ex plainability of the models, patient privacy, and the ethical and social implications. It is concluded that the use of AI and DL can have a positive impact on the care of patients with lung cancer, but further research and regulation are required to ensure its quality and reliability.

</details>


### [97] [GEOSTEER: Geodesic Optimization for Activation Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10658)
*Xuan Cuong Ngo,Hao Vo,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GeoSteer，一种基于黎曼优化的保范激活引导方法，通过自适应多步测地线更新提升大模型控制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保范激活引导方法受限于预定义的引导轨迹和单步更新，难以捕捉激活分布的复杂结构。

Method: 将引导建模为黎曼优化问题，在表示流形上通过一系列小测地线步更新激活；学习非线性激活空间目标函数区分期望与非期望激活，自适应指导每一步。

Result: 在TruthfulQA、RealToxicityPrompts和UltraFeedback基准上，GeoSteer持续优于最先进的激活引导基线。

Conclusion: 用自适应、几何感知的优化替代预定义单步编辑，可使保范引导更有效。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight way to control large language models (LLMs) by modifying their hidden activations at inference time. Among these approaches, norm-preserving steering aims to change model behavior without altering the activation norm, reducing the risk of representation collapse and degradation. However, existing norm-preserving methods are limited by predefined steering trajectories and by their reliance on one-step updates, which may fail to capture the complex structure of activation distributions. We propose GeoSteer, an optimization-based method for norm-preserving activation steering. GeoSteer formulates steering as a Riemannian optimization problem and updates activations through a sequence of small geodesic steps on the representation manifold. To avoid fixed steering directions, GeoSteer learns a nonlinear activation-space objective that distinguishes desired from undesired activations, and uses this function to adaptively guide each steering step. This multistep formulation yields smoother, more stable, and more consistent steering behavior while preserving the activation norm. Across TruthfulQA, RealToxicityPrompts, and UltraFeedback benchmarks, GeoSteer consistently improves over state-of-the-art activation steering baselines. These results suggest that norm-preserving steering can be made more effective by replacing predefined one-step edits with adaptive, geometry-aware optimization.

</details>


### [98] [Conformal Calibration Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10737)
*Achref Doula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究"共形校准迁移"问题：当只有源空间有标注校准数据、目标空间仅有未标注配对观测时，如何实现目标域的覆盖率保证。提出TCC方法，通过配对数据迁移校准并用未标注目标输入进行修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准共形预测依赖校准数据与部署数据之间的可交换性，但实际中该假设常被破坏——标注校准仅存在于源空间，而预测集需用于与源空间通过未标注配对观测（如配对模态或传感器变化）相连的目标空间。因此需要解决无目标标注校准下的校准迁移问题。

Method: 提出Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC)：先用配对数据将源空间标注校准迁移到目标空间，再仅用未标注目标输入修正迁移后的残余失配。给出两种互补实例：TCC-KS，利用无标签不确定性代理检测失配并保守调整校准；weighted-TCC，在权重稳定时对迁移校准重加权以提高效率。

Result: 在CIFAR-100-C、Tiny-ImageNet-C和SEN12MS上验证，无需目标标注校准数据即可实现可靠的目标域覆盖率迁移，且无标签诊断指标能预测何时需要修正。

Conclusion: TCC在缺少目标域标注校准的条件下仍能提供适应可观测失配程度的有限样本目标域覆盖保证，实现了有效的校准迁移。

Abstract: Conformal prediction converts point predictions into set-valued predictions with coverage guarantees under exchangeability between calibration and deployment data. We study conformal calibration transfer, where this requirement fails because labeled calibration is available only in a source space, while prediction sets are needed in a target space linked to the source through unlabeled paired observations (e.g., paired modalities or sensor changes). We propose Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC): we transport labeled source calibration into the target space using the paired data, and then correct residual post-transport mismatch using only unlabeled target inputs. We instantiate this correction with two complementary methods: TCC-KS, which uses a label-free uncertainty surrogate to detect mismatch and adjust calibration conservatively, and weighted-TCC, which reweights transported calibration toward the target domain for improved efficiency when weights are stable. We provide finite-sample target-domain coverage guarantees that adapt to an observable measure of mismatch. Across CIFAR-100-C, Tiny-ImageNet-C, and SEN12MS, we show reliable target-domain coverage transfer without labeled target calibration data, with label-free diagnostics that predict when correction is needed.

</details>


### [99] [The Truth Was Never Gone: Perfect Aliasing in Compliant-Context Truth Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10739)
*Dylan Jayabahu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"完美别名"(perfect aliasing)概念:当真实报告与任务规定动作重合时,在合规情境中拟合的真值探针与规定动作探针解决同一优化问题,无法区分二者 AUROC 加和为 1;通过随机码本与混合情境拟合可将二者分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探针(probe)常用于从模型激活中读出"信念"或"欺骗"等语义属性,但当真实报告与任务规定输出在训练分布中完全重合时,仅凭拟合标签无法确定探针究竟测量的是什么。作者希望揭示这种语义不可识别性,并给出区分真值与规定动作的可行方法。

Method: 在受控二元报告博弈中比较真值探针与规定动作探针的优化目标;用随机码本将规定输出符号与语义动作解耦;再通过在合规情境与对抗情境的混合数据上拟合,把真值与规定动作分离。实验覆盖 751 个 cell-layer 组合,并对一个经奖励训练的 Gemma-2-9B 策略(在所有评估的对抗试验中均给出虚假回答)进行验证,使用三个训练种子评估 AUROC。

Result: 在合规情境下拟合的真值与规定动作探针标签互补,其 AUROC 在所有 751 个 cell-layer 对上严格加和为 1(达到浮点精度)。常规探针在对抗试验上仅得 0.006 ± 0.005 AUROC(三个种子),而混合拟合探针在同一批留出激活上达到 1.000。此外,两个在分布内均完美的合规拟合探针,在同一对抗激活上分别得分 0.080 与 0.986。

Conclusion: 研究发现关乎"探针测量的是什么":完美别名会导致语义不可识别,合规拟合探针无法被解释为真值读出。混合拟合虽实现线性可恢复,但使用了更多样本与带标注对抗情境,因此只能说明线性可分性,不能单独证明去相关带来的收益。作者强调这些结果不建立信念的功能性保留、恢复方向的因果使用,也不构成可部署的欺骗检测器。

Abstract: A truth probe fitted where truthful reporting and a task's prescribed action coincide cannot distinguish those targets from its fitting labels alone. We call this failure of semantic identification perfect aliasing. In a controlled binary reporting game, truth and prescribed-action probes fitted on compliant contexts solve the same optimization. On rival contexts their labels are complements, forcing their AUROCs to sum to one; this identity holds across 751 cell-layer pairs to floating-point precision. We separate prescribed output symbols from semantic action using randomized codebooks, then separate truth from prescribed action by fitting on mixed compliant and rival contexts. For a reward-trained Gemma-2-9B policy that answers falsely on all evaluated rival trials, the conventional probe scores $0.006 \pm 0.005$ AUROC across three training seeds, while mixed-fit probes score $1.000$ on the same held-out activations. Mixed fitting uses more training examples and access to labelled rival contexts, so this comparison establishes linear recoverability rather than isolating the benefit of decorrelation. We also show that two compliant-fit probes, both perfect in-distribution, score $0.080$ and $0.986$ on the same rival activations. The findings concern what a probe measures: they do not establish preserved functional belief, causal use of the recovered direction, or a deployable deception detector. Code and aggregate results accompany the paper.

</details>


### [100] [Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss: Penalizing Center-Class Hedging in Ordinal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10752)
*Manisha Kandel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出自适应边距序数损失（AMOL）以抑制标准交叉熵在序数分类中的“中心类对冲”问题，并在多个基准上取得最优QWK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵损失在序数分类任务中会使网络将预测向中间类别靠拢，即“中心类对冲”。这是因为预测中间类能最小化期望对称损失，成为无论真实标签如何的“最省力路径”。现有序数损失虽能处理大误差惩罚、排序一致性等问题，但都没有针对真实标签相对序数中心位置来直接抑制中心类对冲。

Method: 作者提出AMOL，对逐类损失项施加乘法权重 m(k,y)=1+α·(1-|k-c|/c)·(|y-c|/c)，其中c为中心类、k为候选类、y为真实标签。该权重编码联合条件：仅当候选类接近中心且真实标签远离中心时才较大，其他情况退化为标准行为。此外提出中心对冲率（CHR）作为直接量化该失效模式的诊断指标。

Result: 在四个序数分类基准、五个随机种子上，AMOL在所有四个数据集上取得最优或并列最优的二次加权Kappa（QWK），优于交叉熵、OLL和SORD基线。非对称变体AMOL-asym在Abalone数据集上完全消除中心类对冲（CHR=0.000±0.000，五个种子均如此，每次运行约266个极端类测试样本），而标准交叉熵为0.074±0.005。

Conclusion: AMOL通过联合条件权重有效抑制中心类对冲，配合CHR诊断指标，在多个序数分类基准上实现最优或并列最优性能，非对称变体更能完全消除该失效模式。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy loss causes neural networks trained on ordinal classification tasks to hedge predictions toward center classes, a failure mode we term \emph{center-class hedging}. This occurs because predicting the middle class minimizes expected symmetric loss, making it the path of least resistance regardless of the true label. Existing ordinal losses address related problems such as large-error penalization and rank consistency, but none directly suppresses center-class hedging as a function of where the true label lies relative to the ordinal center. We propose the Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss (AMOL), a multiplicative weight applied to per-class loss terms of the form $m(k,y) = 1 + α\cdot (1 - |k-c|/c) \cdot (|y-c|/c)$, where $c$ is the center class, $k$ is the candidate class, and $y$ is the true label. The weight encodes a joint condition: it is large only when the candidate class is near center and the true label is far from center, collapsing to standard behavior otherwise. We further introduce the Center-Hedging Rate (CHR) as a diagnostic metric that directly quantifies this failure mode. Across four ordinal classification benchmarks and five random seeds, AMOL achieves the best or tied-best Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) on all four datasets compared to cross-entropy, OLL, and SORD baselines. An asymmetric variant (AMOL-asym) eliminates center-class hedging entirely on the Abalone dataset ($\text{CHR} = 0.000 \pm 0.000$ across all five seeds, $n \approx 266$ extreme-class test samples per run), compared to $0.074 \pm 0.005$ for standard cross-entropy.

</details>


### [101] [A Bellman Optimality Equation for Plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10776)
*Jeremy Lucas,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文初步探索在持续强化学习中优化"可塑性"，并证明存在类似赋权优化的Bellman最优方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续强化学习中稳定性-可塑性的权衡是核心难题。Abel等人(2025)将权衡重新表述为赋权-可塑性权衡，但尚无可塑性优化的研究，本文旨在填补此空白。

Method: 在马尔可夫决策过程框架下，研究如何优化由观测到动作的广义有向信息所定义的可塑性，并推导相应的Bellman最优方程。

Result: 证明了存在一个用于优化可塑性的Bellman最优方程，与先前赋权优化的结果相类似。

Conclusion: 本文为在赋权-可塑性新定义下优化可塑性提供了初步理论基础，未来可在此基础上发展具体算法。

Abstract: In continual reinforcement learning, carefully managing the stability-plasticity tradeoff remains a core challenge. Recent work by Abel et al. (2025) formalized this dilemma by defining plasticity as the generalized directed information from an agent's observations to its actions, and empowerment as the generalized directed information from its actions to its observations. This formulation successfully reframes the traditional stability-plasticity tradeoff as an empowerment-plasticity tradeoff. However, while extensive literature exists on optimizing for empowerment, there is currently no research addressing the optimization of plasticity under this new definition. This paper presents preliminary work toward optimizing plasticity within Markov decision processes. We show that there exists a Bellman optimality equation for optimizing plasticity similar to previous work for empowerment.

</details>


### [102] [Counterfactual Marginalisation: Framework for Evaluating Robustness to Nuisance Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10778)
*Yasin Ibrahim,Hermione Warr,Robin J. Evans,Konstantinos Kamnitsas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出反事实边缘化（CF marginalisation）这一测试时评估方法，用于衡量分类模型对人口统计或采集相关捷径变量的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型可能在测试中表现优异，却依赖人口统计或采集相关的捷径（shortcut），而非真正学到目标特征。因此需要一种能够在测试时评估模型对这类变量鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 给定一个反事实图像生成器，对年龄、性别等"干扰"父变量进行干预，为每张测试图像生成反事实版本，并在目标干预分布上对预测取平均；由此得到"干预感知"的预测，在保留患者特定潜在信息的同时边缘化人口统计效应。基于该预测定义反事实风险、校准、稳定性与最坏情况敏感度等指标。

Result: 实验展示了该框架在定量鲁棒性评估方面的实用性。

Conclusion: 反事实边缘化为评估分类模型对人口统计等捷径变量的鲁棒性提供了一种可行的测试时评估程序。

Abstract: Machine learning models can achieve strong test performance while relying on demographic or acquisition-related shortcuts. We propose counterfactual (CF) marginalisation as a test-time evaluation procedure for assessing robustness of classification models to such variables. Given a CF image generator, we intervene on nuisance parent variables such as age or sex, generate CF versions of each test image, and average predictions over a target intervention distribution. This produces intervention-aware predictions that marginalise demographic effects while preserving patient-specific latent information. We use these predictions to define metrics for CF risk, calibration, stability and worst-case sensitivity. We demonstrate this framework's utility for quantitative robustness evaluation.

</details>


### [103] [From Connectivity to Rewards: Dense Reward Learning with Directed State Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10781)
*Shuyuan Zhang,Zihan Wang,Xiao-Wen Chang,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出G2QDR框架，通过状态连通性模型预测非对称环境中的成对状态连通强度，并将其转化为标量辅助稠密奖励，为分层强化学习提供跨层级连续引导，可理论集成到任意GCHRL架构中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将图与目标条件分层强化学习（GCHRL）结合的方法忽视了图的内在连通性信息，仅将图作为随机采样工具，而非编码状态连通性访问信息的环境模型。这一问题在准度量（quasimetric）环境中尤为突出，因为状态转移的固有非对称性给稳定策略学习和鲁棒路径规划带来根本挑战。

Method: 提出状态连通性模型，预测非对称环境中成对状态的连通强度；将这些连通强度转化为标量辅助稠密奖励，提供跨多个分层层级的连续引导。该状态连通性模型通过在有向状态图上训练的神经网络高效实现，其中有向图在探索过程中生成。框架（G2QDR）在理论上可集成到任意现有GCHRL架构中。

Result: 在多种稀疏奖励环境上的实证结果表明，G2QDR总体上能提升基线GCHRL方法的性能，且计算开销可接受。

Conclusion: G2QDR通过利用有向状态图的连通性信息生成稠密奖励，解决了准度量环境中非对称状态转移的挑战，能通用地增强现有GCHRL方法，具有良好的可扩展性与较低的额外计算成本。

Abstract: The integration of graphs with Goal-Conditioned Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (GCHRL) has received increasing attention, as graphs naturally encode task hierarchies for effective subgoal sampling. However, existing methods often overlook intrinsic connectivity information, failing to fully leverage the underlying topology for efficient learning. Most graph-based GCHRL methods use the graph as a stochastic sampling tool rather than as an environmental model that encodes connectivity and state-accessibility information. This limitation is particularly acute in quasimetric environments, where the inherent asymmetry of state transitions poses a fundamental challenge to stable policy learning and robust path planning. In this paper, we address these problems by introducing a state connectivity model designed to predict pairwise state connectivity strength in asymmetric environments. We transform these connectivity strengths into scalar auxiliary dense rewards, providing continuous guidance across multiple hierarchical levels. We demonstrate that our proposed framework, Graph-Guided Quasimetric Dense Reward (G2QDR), can theoretically be integrated into any existing GCHRL architecture, and the state connectivity model is efficiently implemented via a neural network trained on a directed state graph generated during exploration. Empirical results across a wide range of sparse reward environments indicate that, in general, G2QDR can enhance the performance of baseline GCHRL approaches with acceptable computational overhead.

</details>


### [104] [DR-LabStack: Design and Implementation of a Clinician-Facing Web System for Diabetic Retinopathy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10796)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文设计并实现了 DR-LabStack，一个基于 React-Flask 的 Web 系统，用于整合四个异构的糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）预训练模型，并对其软件功能进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DR 预训练模型在输入字段、序列化格式、预处理需求和输出语义上各不相同，难以通过统一临床界面访问；因此需要显式协调用户界面与推理服务。

Method: 构建 React-Flask Web 系统，集成 RuleFit、Pruned RuleFit、Elaborative XGBoost 和 Two-level Ensemble 四个模型；通过共享表单检索有序特征、渲染数值与类别控件、构建位置输入向量，后端适配器加载异构工件并应用集成模型配套的 scaler，统一 JSON 响应支持二分类展示。

Result: 功能评估显示四个模型成功加载并暴露 14、6、8、25 字段契约；62 次 Flask 测试客户端请求刻画服务行为，12 次有限向量检查确认调用路径与阈值一致性，24 个浏览器组件场景验证输入排序、结果渲染及输入校验行为。

Conclusion: 系统展示了面向异构 DR 模型的可复用交互与服务流程，贡献在于 Web 系统设计、集成与软件功能；临床有效性与临床医生可用性仍需单独评估。

Abstract: Pretrained diabetic retinopathy (DR) prediction models differ in their input fields, serialization formats, preprocessing requirements, and output semantics. Making these models accessible through a common clinical interface therefore requires explicit coordination between the user interface and the inference service. We designed and implemented DR-LabStack, a React-Flask web system integrating four externally developed pretrained models: RuleFit, Pruned RuleFit, Elaborative XGBoost, and Two-level Ensemble. A shared form retrieves ordered model features, renders model-specific numerical and categorical controls, and constructs a positional input vector. Backend adapters load heterogeneous artifacts and apply the ensemble's accompanying scaler, while a common JSON response supports binary classification display alongside method and source information. Functional evaluation on September 8, 2026 used copied application files and real model artifacts in a documented isolated environment. All four models loaded and exposed their 14-, 6-, 8-, and 25-field contracts. Sixty-two Flask test-client requests characterized service behavior; 12 limited-vector checks confirmed invocation-path and threshold consistency. Twenty-four browser-component scenarios with mocked transport verified input ordering and result rendering and characterized input-validation behavior. The resulting system demonstrates a reusable interaction and serving workflow for heterogeneous DR models. The contribution is web-system design, integration, and software functionality; clinical effectiveness and clinician usability require separate evaluation.

</details>


### [105] [RiVaT-Fuse: Reliability-Calibrated Variational Tensor Fusion for Multimodal Prediction under Modality Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10798)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang,Taiping Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RiVaT-Fuse，将图像-元数据融合定义为样本级隐状态估计的可靠性校准变分张量融合框架，在图像级基准上取得最强的整体预测排名并提升扰动下的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像-元数据预测需要融合可靠性随样本和潜在因素变化的异构证据。现有表示级融合方法通常直接选择拼接、门控、条件调制或注意力等聚合结构，却没有明确定义在模态不确定性下融合表示应当意味着什么。

Method: RiVaT-Fuse通过变分目标估计一致性隐状态，该目标平衡图像证据、元数据证据、结构化跨模态交互和稳定性，而非直接聚合生成融合向量。它以矩阵值信任几何替代标量模态置信度，将交互分解为加性、乘性和关系性成分，并将隐状态与条件鲁棒性及结构化多任务预测耦合。文中给出隐状态求解的适定性与稳定性解释，并用高效的低秩加对角信任算子实例化该框架。

Result: 在图像级图像-元数据预测基准上，RiVaT-Fuse在直接表示级基线中取得最强的整体预测排名，同时在扰动下改善了概率与标签稳定性。

Conclusion: 将融合重新表述为可靠性校准的样本级隐状态估计，并引入矩阵值信任几何与结构化交互分解，是一种有效且更稳定的图像-元数据融合范式。

Abstract: Image-metadata prediction requires fusing heterogeneous evidence whose reliability can vary across samples and latent factors. Existing representation-level fusion methods typically choose an aggregation architecture, such as concatenation, gating, conditional modulation, or attention, without explicitly defining what the fused representation should mean under modality uncertainty. We propose RiVaT-Fuse, a reliability-calibrated variational tensor fusion framework that defines fusion as sample-wise latent-state estimation. Rather than producing a fused vector by direct aggregation, RiVaT-Fuse estimates a consensus latent state through a variational objective that balances image evidence, metadata evidence, structured cross-modal interaction, and stability. The resulting framework replaces scalar modality confidence with matrix-valued trust geometry, decomposes interaction into additive, multiplicative, and relational components, and couples the latent state with conditional robustness and structured multi-task prediction. We provide well-posedness and stability interpretations of the latent solve and instantiate the framework with efficient low-rank-plus-diagonal trust operators. On an image-level image-metadata prediction benchmark, RiVaT-Fuse achieves the strongest overall predictive rank among direct representation-level baselines while improving probability and label stability under perturbation.

</details>


### [106] [Processing and classifying bird songs using wavelet techniques and supervised learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10826)
*Laura Lucia Dominguez Barrios,Fidel Aniano Causil Barrios,Alex Rodrigo dos Santos Sousa,Mariana Rodrigues Motta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Epanechnikov核先验贝叶斯小波收缩的降噪框架，用于高噪声自然声景中入侵鸟种鸣声的分类，SVM在10维MFCC下准确率最高达0.9398。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然声景中环境噪声高，导致鸟鸣信号退化，影响入侵鸟种自动监测与生物入侵管理的效果。

Method: 采用基于Epanechnikov核先验的贝叶斯小波收缩方法（具闭式决策规则、计算高效）对iNaturalist上三种鸟类（Euphonia violacea、Leiothrix lutea、Passer domesticus）录音降噪；随后提取MFCC及熵、过零率等频谱特征；比较Random Forest、Multinomial Logistic Regression与SVM在不同特征维度下的表现。

Result: 小波预处理显著提升分类性能，SVM在10维MFCC配置下取得最高准确率0.9398。

Conclusion: 该研究为生态自动监测与生物入侵管理提供了稳健的统计工具。

Abstract: This study proposes an integrated framework for the processing and classification of invasive bird species vocalizations within natural soundscapes, characterized by high levels of environmental noise. We address the challenge of signal degradation by employing a Bayesian wavelet shrinkage methodology based on the Epanechnikov kernel prior, which offers a closed form decision rule and high computational efficiency for processing large bioacoustic datasets. The methodology was applied to recordings of three species obtained from the iNaturalist platform: \textit{Euphonia violacea}, \textit{Leiothrix lutea}, and \textit{Passer domesticus}. After signal denoising, we extracted a comprehensive set of features, including Mel-Frequency Cepstral Coefficients (MFCCs) and spectral indices such as entropy and zero-crossing rate.
